### [1] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多工具图像编辑归属（MIEA）任务，用于从多步骤编辑的人脸图像中识别所涉及的多个编辑工具。作者构建了包含50万+编辑人脸图像的新数据集MultiEdit，覆盖换脸（Deepfake）及多种面部增强等六类编辑工具，并提出DPEC方法，结合空间与频率域的局部感知工具痕迹捕获及基于错误的课程学习策略，在最多五步编辑的图像上优于九种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归属方法基于单一工具假设，只能识别单个编辑工具，无法应对现实中日益常见的多工具编辑场景——其中不同编辑工具留下的痕迹是复合且重叠的，给归属任务带来挑战。

Method: 首先构建MultiEdit数据集，包含50万+张经过多种工具组合编辑的人脸图像，覆盖换脸与面部增强等六类编辑工具；随后设计DPEC方法，从空间域和频率域捕获具有区分性、局部感知的编辑工具痕迹，并结合基于误差的课程学习策略进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，DPEC在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上，性能优于九种基线方法，验证了其多工具归属的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索了多工具图像编辑归属问题，验证了从空间与频率域提取工具痕迹并结合课程学习的可行性，为该方向提供了新的数据集基准与方法框架。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Textual Chain-of-Thought: A Survey on Action-Grounded Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01659)
*Zhengxu Tang,Xiaozhou Zhang,Guofeng Cui,Ziyu Gong,Zi Wang,Yunfei Shi,Ruifeng Deng,Chengzhi Qi,Ke Chen,Sachin Patil,Tianjun Xiao,Langechuan Liu,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自动驾驶中接地推理（action-grounded reasoning）的综述。作者调研了171篇论文（含130篇方法论文和41篇基准/数据集/综述/分析论文），提出了一种以表征为中心的、将中间状态形式作为组织轴心的分类法，将130种方法系统化为四类：基于语言的推理、视觉空间推理、潜在动态推理和外部化推理，并进一步细分为13个子类型。综合发现表明，驾驶智能体推理的开放前沿在于能够接地于真实世界、与实时动作耦合且在安全关键系统下可验证的中间表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统链式思维（CoT）推理通过生成中间步骤来增强生成模型的能力，但在自动驾驶中，最终输出是连续动作，其推理必须与物理世界的时空结构保持一致。这一根本性差异驱动了对从文本CoT向动作接地推理转变的深入研究，现有综述缺乏对这一转变的系统化梳理。

Method: 作者调查了171篇论文，提出以表征为中心的分类法，以中间状态的形式作为组织类别的主轴，将130种方法分为四类（语言、视觉空间、潜在动态、外部化推理）并细分为13个与不同感兴趣区域相关联的子类型。

Result: 系统化梳理了130种方法并归纳为四个大类、13个子类型；分析指出推理与短期推理在存在动作接地前提下的差异，指出了当前开放的前沿领域是需要在真实世界中可接地、可与实时动作耦合、并在安全关键系统约束下可验证的中间表征。

Conclusion: 驾驶智能体推理的未来方向在于开发既能接地于物理世界、又能与实时动作时序耦合、且可在安全关键系统中被验证的中间表征，这将是从语言为主的CoT范式走向真正动作接地推理的关键。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning powers generative models by eliciting intermediate steps before producing an answer. In autonomous driving, the answer is a continuous action. Thus its reasoning must share the same spatiotemporal structure as the physical world. This survey studies the resulting shift from textual CoT to action-grounded reasoning. Surveying 171 papers, including 130 method papers and 41 benchmarks, datasets, surveys, and analysis papers, we propose a representation-centered taxonomy that treats the form of the intermediate state as the organizing axis. We systematize the 130 methods into four categories: language-based, visual-spatial, latent-dynamic, and externalized reasoning, further divided into 13 subtypes tied to distinct regions of interests. Our synthesis shows that the open frontier of reasoning in driving agents lies in intermediate representations that can be grounded in the real world, coupled to real-time action, and verified under safety-critical systems. Project page: https://github.com/tangzhengxu/awesome-av-cot.

</details>


### [3] [FORGE: Forward-Only Test-Time Adaptation for Integer-Only Vision Models on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01683)
*Muhammad Rehan,Haider Ali,Muhammad Ali Munir,Moaz Amjad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯前向的测试时自适应（TTA）方法，可在部署于微控制器上的BN折叠整数卷积网络上运行，通过重归一化每个折叠卷积的逐通道输出到干净训练统计量来恢复自适应能力，在ESP32-S3上仅需8.3 mJ和21.9 ms即可完成自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在MCU上的视觉模型通常量化至纯整数运算，且推理时运行时不支持反向传播，因此无法使用标准的梯度基测试时自适应（TTA）来适应部署环境中的分布偏移（如传感器噪声、模糊、光照变化）。现有前向TTA方法要么仅适用于服务器或边缘GPU级模型，要么依赖被整数部署融合掉的批归一化（BN）层，缺乏针对真实微控制器整数执行的解决方案。

Method: 核心观察是：将BN融合进前面的卷积层（整数推理的必需步骤）会破坏归一化自适应所需的统计信息。作者通过仅使用前向传播估计，将每个融合卷积的逐通道输出重新归一化到其干净的训练统计量来恢复自适应能力。方法设计包括：批量大小缩放的动量以支持单样本流式推理、仅有选择性的层自适应（无需查看测试污染即可选出少量关键层）。

Result: 该方法恢复了梯度基TENT大部分精度提升（+20.9 vs +24.9点），与纯前向BN自适应性能相当，且是唯一能在折叠整数模型上运行的方法；仅需自适应21层中的3层即可获得93%的收益；支持批量大小为1的流式推理；在三个数据集（最多200类）和两种架构上均有效。在ESP32-S3上的实测中（PPK2功耗分析仪），自适应仅耗8.3 mJ（推理能耗的6.8%）和21.9 ms。

Conclusion: 前向自适应在真实微控制器上是廉价的。该方法证明了即使在不支持反向传播、BN已折叠的整数推理环境中，仍可通过巧妙的统计重归一化实现有效的测试时自适应，为边缘设备上的鲁棒视觉部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision models deployed on microcontrollers (MCUs) are quantized to integer-only arithmetic and run in inference-only runtimes that do not carry the machinery backpropagation needs: the standard tool for adapting a model to the distribution shift (sensor noise, blur, lighting) it meets in the field. Existing forward-only test-time adaptation (TTA) methods either run only on server- or edge-GPU-class models (not true microcontroller integer execution), or require the batch-normalization (BN) layers that integer deployment fuses away. We present a forward-only TTA method that operates on deployed, BN-folded, integer-only convolutional networks. The key observation is that fusing BN into the preceding convolution, a mandatory step for integer inference, destroys the statistics that normalization-based adaptation relies on. We restore adaptation by re-normalizing each folded convolution's per-channel output to its clean training statistics, using only forward-pass estimates. The method (i) recovers most of gradient-based TENT's accuracy gain (+20.9 vs. +24.9 points) and matches forward-only BN adaptation, while being the only method that runs on a folded integer-only model; (ii) needs to adapt only 3 of 21 layers (selected without seeing the test corruptions) to recover 93% of the benefit; (iii) survives single-sample streaming with a batch-size-scaled momentum; and (iv) generalizes across three datasets (up to 200 classes) and two architectures. We validate bit-exact int8 convolution execution and deploy on an ESP32-S3, where, measured with a Nordic PPK2 power profiler, the forward-only adaptation (a lightweight fp32 recalibration around the int8 convolutions) costs only 8.3 mJ (6.8% of inference energy) and 21.9 ms on the deployed SIMD-optimized model: forward-only adaptation is cheap on a real microcontroller.

</details>


### [4] [FairLens: Benchmarking Fairness in Vision-Language Models for High-Stakes Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01691)
*Vahid Reza Khazaie,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FAIRLENS基准与评估框架，用于衡量视觉语言模型（VLMs）在招聘、法律和医疗三个高风险领域中响应的公平性与有效性。通过真实人脸图像配对不同人口统计属性，从四个互补视角评估八个VLM，发现模型主要失败在于无根据的推断（从面部推断资质、威胁、疾病或职业角色）而非不平等对待，这类失败在法律和医疗领域最为严重，且单纯的差异指标无法捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM越来越多地被用于基于视觉输入的高风险决策，其公平性和响应有效性缺乏系统评估。现有评估仅关注群组间差异（如差异指标），忽略了"无证据时是否拒绝推断"这一核心有效性问题，导致存在严重的有害推断却未被发现。

Method: 构建FAIRLENS基准：(1) 将覆盖性别、种族、年龄组的真实人脸图像与封闭式和开放式问题配对，每个模型生成超过10万对图像-问题对；(2) 从四个互补视角评估：不良结果率的人口统计差异、合理性（soundness，即是否基于问题证据作答且无证据时弃权）、对不支持的职业和地位的人口统计关联、自由文本生成中的偏见；(3) 评估八个VLM。

Result: 主要失败模式是"无根据推断"而非"不平等对待"：模型经常从面部推断资质、威胁、疾病或职业角色而非弃权，最弱模型在99%的不可回答问题上仍进行推断。这些失败在法律和医疗领域最严重。人口差异指标存在盲区：绝对差异虽小，但基线不良率低时该差异意味着某一群体被贴上不良标签的频率高出数倍；且小差异也可能反映模型对所有群体都不安全。自由文本偏见与多项选择准确率仅松散耦合。

Conclusion: FAIRLENS表明，高风险场景下VLM的公平行为需要同时满足：跨群体的类似处理（公平性）以及拒绝从外表推断高维属性（有效性）。该基准的问题套件可迁移到任何具有人口统计标注的人脸语料库。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to make decisions from visual inputs. We introduce FAIRLENS, a benchmark and evaluation framework for measuring both the fairness and the validity of VLM responses in three high-stakes domains: hiring, legal, and healthcare. FAIRLENS pairs real face images spanning gender, race, and age groups with closed- and open-ended questions, giving more than 100K image-question pairs per model, and evaluates responses from four complementary views: demographic parity over adverse outcome rates, soundness, demographic association over unsupported roles and statuses, and bias in free-text generation. Soundness is the central validity criterion: a response is sound when it follows the evidence stated in the question and abstains when the image cannot support an answer. Evaluating eight VLMs, we find that the primary failure is unwarranted inference rather than unequal treatment. Models routinely infer qualifications, threat, illness, or professional role from a face instead of abstaining, and the weakest model does so on 99% of the questions its input cannot answer. These failures are most severe in legal and healthcare, where recognizing insufficient evidence matters most, and disparity metrics alone would miss them: parity gaps are small in absolute terms, yet when baseline adverse rates are low the same gap means one demographic group receives adverse labels several times as often as another, and a small gap can equally reflect a model that treats every group unsafely. Bias in free-text responses is only loosely coupled to multiple-choice accuracy, so correct structured answers do not imply safe generation. FAIRLENS shows that fair high-stakes VLM behavior requires similar treatment across groups and refusal to infer high-stakes attributes from appearance, and its question suite transfers to any face corpus with demographic annotations.

</details>


### [5] [From Visual Cues to Spoken Narration: Rethinking Audio Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01725)
*Akshita Gupta,Aditya Arora,Federico Tombari,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cue2Narrate，一个两阶段流水线用于在长未修剪电影片段中联合预测音频描述的"内容"（何时叙述哪个视觉事件）和"时机"（插入位置）。提出LongLSMDC基准（平均6.5分钟片段）用于多段AD生成评估，实验显示Cue2Narrate在定位和生成任务上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述（AD）对视觉障碍观众至关重要，但现有研究大多将其简化为预先分割视频片段的视频字幕生成，忽略了"何时叙述"这一关键问题。作者希望解决真实场景中更长、未修剪电影片段的联合"内容+时机"预测问题。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线Cue2Narrate：(1) 双头音视频定位器预测每个AD话语的两个时间窗口——视觉线索窗口和口头叙述窗口；(2) LoRA适配的VLM利用预测的视觉证据生成简洁AD，并使用描述排序损失将相同帧的负样本字幕排在真实AD之后进行训练。

Result: 引入LongLSMDC基准（最长8分钟片段，平均6.5分钟）。在LongLSMDC上，Cue2Narrate在平均mAP上比仅视频和仅音频定位基线高出5-12个百分点。在预测窗口和真实窗口评估下，AD生成性能均超过相应微调的基础VLM。

Conclusion: Cue2Narrate首次建立了长片段多段AD生成的基准，证明联合建模"何时"和"什么"的定位与生成两阶段方案在长未修剪电影片段上有效可行。

Abstract: Audio Description (AD) provides spoken narration of visual events during dialogue gaps, making movies accessible to visually impaired audiences. The problem requires determining both what (which visual event) and when (position for inserting the AD) to narrate, to achieve the best user experience. Prior work has largely reduced the problem to video captioning of pre-segmented video clips, i.e., what is largely predefined and when is ignored entirely. We propose Cue2Narrate, a two-stage pipeline that jointly predicts what and when to narrate in longer untrimmed movie clips. A dual-head audio-visual localizer predicts two temporally distinct windows per AD utterance: a visual cue window and a spoken narration window. A LoRA-adapted VLM then generates concise ADs from the predicted visual evidence, trained with a Description Ranking Loss that ranks captions (negative samples) of the same frames lower than the GT AD. To benchmark this new problem statement, we introduce the LongLSMDC benchmark with up to 8-min movie clips (~6.5min on average). On LongLSMDC, Cue2Narrate outperforms video-only and audio-only localization baselines by 5--12 points in avg. mAP. Under both predicted- and GT-window evaluation, Cue2Narrate improves AD generation over the corresponding fine-tuned base VLM. These results establish the first benchmark for multi-segment AD generation on long-form clips. Data & Code: https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/Cue2Narrate

</details>


### [6] [UAV Thermal Imagery for Inert Ordnance Screening: Multi Campaign Dataset Development,Object Detection, and Practical Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01738)
*Chad Melton,PhD.,Annabelle Kelton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究创建了多场次无人机热成像未爆弹药（UXO）数据集，测试了YOLOV11l和RT-DETR-R50目标检测模型，并提出了人道主义排雷应用中关于数据采集、模型校准和人工审核的实际建议，模型旨在用于筛选和优先级排序而非独立排雷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未爆弹药在世界多地持续限制平民活动、农业、基础设施恢复和环境修复，需要开发自动化目标检测方法来辅助人道主义排雷工作，提高探测效率和安全性。

Method: 研究在田纳西州进行了四次野外采集活动，涵盖夏冬两季，将惰性弹药放置在短草、高植被、砾石、覆盖物、岩石、堆肥和压实地面等多种表面，在33米和15米飞行高度下采集热成像数据，构建了5,855对热像标注数据集，并训练评估了YOLOV11l和RT-DETR-R50两种检测算法。

Result: 最终形成了包含918张正样本图像和4,937张背景图像的数据集；33米数据集含420张训练图和106张验证图，15米数据集含629张训练图和157张验证图；两种模型均成功完成训练评估，并总结出多项实用建议。

Conclusion: 该系统适用于后续技术勘测或爆炸物处理（EOD）评估的筛选和优先排序，而非独立排雷的依据；建议同时采集热像与RGB影像、纳入多种地表和纯背景图像、考虑太阳照射变化后的时段、平衡勘测覆盖与目标像素表征、用本地数据校准模型并保留人工审核环节。

Abstract: Unexploded ordnance (UXO) continues to restrict civilian access, agricultural activity, infrastructure recovery, and environmental remediation in contaminated areas around the world. This study created a multi campaign UAV thermal image data set of inert ordnance, developed a labeled image set from collected imagery, tested object detection models, and identified practical considerations for humanitarian mine action and demining applications. Data were collected during four field campaigns in Tennessee under summer and winter conditions using inert mines, munitions, and other ordnance placed in short grass, tall vegetation, gravel, mulch, rock, compost, and compacted surfaces. Thermal imagery was collected under flight altitutes of 33 m and 15 m. The final source inventory contained 5,855 thermal image label pairs, including 918 positive images and 4,937 background images. After retaining all positive images and downsampling background images, the 33 m dataset contained 420 training and 106 validation images, while the 15 m dataset contained 629 training and 157 validation images. YOLOV11l and RT-DETR-R50 algorithms were trained and evaluated to develop an automated candidate detection model. Practical recommendations include collecting thermal and RGB imagery together, incorporating varied surfaces and background only imagery, considering periods following changes in solar exposure, balancing survey coverage against target pixel representation, calibrating models with representative local data, and retaining qualified human review. The intended use is screening and prioritization for follow on technical survey or EOD assessment, and not a standalone clearance.

</details>


### [7] [ZipTok3D: High-Fidelity 3D Tokenization with Compact Token Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01740)
*Mingda Lin,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Bowen Cui,Yefei He,Haoyu Zhao,Yuanyu He,Donny Y. Chen,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ZipTok3D，一种面向极短token序列的3D tokenizer，通过嵌套丢弃（nested dropout）将几何信息按优先级组织到前缀token中，并利用参数共享的Transformer解码器迭代展开，实现极低token预算下的高保真重建，单token即可达到COD-VAE 32-token的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D tokenizer在极低token预算下重建质量急剧下降，无论是空间区域式还是全局固定尺寸式的潜在表示都难以在极压缩情况下保持高保真度，需要一种能够从极短token序列中高效重建3D几何的方法。

Method: ZipTok3D的核心思想是将对象几何组织为渐进信息丰富的全局token前缀，并通过迭代解码展开这些紧凑表示。具体方法包括：(1) 训练时通过嵌套丢弃（nested dropout）随机截断编码后的潜在序列，要求每个保留的前缀都能重构完整对象，从而使关键几何信息优先存储在前导token中；(2) 解码器通过参数共享的Transformer块，从每个前缀中恢复细粒度几何，无需独立的生成采样阶段。

Result: 在相同token维度下，ZipTok3D仅用1个token即可在ShapeNet上达到与32-token COD-VAE基线相当的重建质量，用4个token在TRELLIS上达到同等质量，分别实现了32倍和8倍的token序列压缩。

Conclusion: ZipTok3D通过前缀式几何信息组织和迭代解码策略，有效解决了极低token预算下3D重建质量严重退化的问题，显著缩短了3D生成所需的token序列长度，为高效紧凑的3D表示提供了新思路。

Abstract: Compact token sequences are essential for efficient 3D generation. However, existing 3D tokenizers typically organize latent representations either over spatial regions or as fixed-size sets of global tokens, both suffering sharp reconstruction degradation when compressed to extremely low token budgets. In this paper, we present ZipTok3D, a 3D tokenizer designed for high-fidelity reconstruction from extremely short token sequences. Its key idea is to organize object geometry into progressively informative global-token prefixes and unfold these compact representations through iterative decoding. Specifically, nested dropout randomly truncates the latent sequence after encoding during training and requires each retained prefix to reconstruct the complete object, thereby prioritizing essential geometric information in the leading tokens. The decoder then repeatedly applies a parameter-shared Transformer block to recover fine-grained geometry from each prefix without a separate generative sampling stage. With the same token dimension, ZipTok3D achieves reconstruction quality comparable to the 32-token COD-VAE baseline using only one token on ShapeNet and four on TRELLIS, yielding $32\times$ and $8\times$ shorter token sequences, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Evidential Deep Learning for Multi-Modal Anti-UAV Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01742)
*Dmitry Golovchits,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验评估三种不确定性方案（EDL头、DS证据融合、不确定性驱动的时间传感器门控）在反无人机检测中的效果。结果显示EDL训练目标显著提升精度（E1中准确率+5.9个百分点、跟踪器缺席率翻三倍；E2中分类准确率+4.8个百分点，p=0.011），且能更好排序分类误差；但DS融合不优于简单概率平均，Dirichlet空乏在检测层面失效，时间门控仅在几乎不激活时保持精度且无实际延迟节省。EDL收益主要来自训练目标而非其不确定性估计，检测层面的失败可定位至锚点级评估而非学习表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反无人机系统日益融合多传感器，但检测头缺乏各模态可靠性信号，现有研究对EDL、DS证据融合和不确定性驱动门控的实际效果缺乏严格的受控验证。作者旨在通过消融实验严格评估这些方法是否真正优于传统基线。

Method: 在三个基准（AntiUAV600热跟踪、TRIDENT RGB-音频-RF分类、MM-UAV RGB-IR跟踪）上进行受控消融：比较EDL训练目标与重训练的sigmoid基线，评估DS融合与概率平均值，检验Dirichlet空乏与预测熵的排序能力，测试不确定性驱动的时间传感器门控，并引入裁剪级对照实验定位检测层面的失败来源。

Result: EDL训练目标在E1中准确率+5.9个百分点且跟踪器缺席率翻三倍，E2中分类准确率+4.8个百分点（p=0.011，通过裁剪聚类bootstrap验证）；熵UAUC约0.94 vs 0.51。但DS融合不优于简单平均；Dirichlet空乏在检测层面反转，与预测熵一样在极端背景不平衡下编码类别归属而非错误可能性；时间门控几乎不激活时才能保持精度且无实际延迟节省。

Conclusion: EDL在反无人机任务中的收益主要来自其训练目标而非不确定性估计本身。检测层面上的不确定性方法失效可归因于锚点级评估方式而非学习表示；DS融合、空乏不确定性排序和时间门控提出的假设均未得到实验支持。

Abstract: Anti-UAV systems increasingly fuse multiple sensors, yet their detection heads provide no per-modality reliability signal. This study evaluates whether evidential deep learning (EDL) heads, Dempster-Shafer (DS) evidence fusion, and uncertainty-driven temporal sensor gating improve anti-UAV detection through a controlled ablation on three benchmarks: thermal tracking (AntiUAV600), RGB-audio-RF classification (TRIDENT), and RGB-IR tracking (MM-UAV). The EDL training objective improves accuracy over retrained sigmoid baselines (+5.9 percentage points in accuracy and a tripled tracker-on-absent rate in E1; +4.8 percentage points in classification accuracy in E2, surviving a clip-clustered bootstrap, p = 0.011) and ranks classification errors substantially better (entropy UAUC approximately 0.94 vs. 0.51). The remaining components do not support their respective hypotheses. DS fusion does not outperform simple probability averaging. Dirichlet vacuity adds no ranking power beyond predictive entropy and inverts at the detection level, where extreme background imbalance causes it to encode class membership rather than error likelihood, a failure also observed for entropy and sigmoid confidence. Temporal gating preserves accuracy only when nearly inactive and yields no realised latency saving on shared-backbone hardware. The benefit of evidential learning therefore arises primarily from its training objective rather than its uncertainty estimate; a crop-level control further localises the detection-level breakdown to anchor-level evaluation rather than the learned representation.

</details>


### [9] [SCULPT: Training Edge Vision Models for Post-Training Quantization Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01743)
*Bharadwaj Kavuri,Sourav Babu-PK,Varadhraj Ellapan,Pullarao Maddu,Prasad Deshpande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT是一种训练时方法，通过在普通FP32微调过程中优化激活分布（抑制偏斜度和峰度）并学习可部署的百分位裁剪边界，提升边缘视觉模型在低比特后训练量化（PTQ）中的就绪度，而无需量化感知训练或运行时激活变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视觉模型在资源受限硬件上部署困难，低比特PTQ具有吸引力。但FP32训练产生的激活分布常呈重尾形态，离群值破坏激活量化稳定性：保留完整范围浪费量化位在罕见极值上，激进裁剪又造成信息损失。现有方案如QAT增加训练复杂度和位宽耦合，后处理PTQ方法则需运行时修复。

Method: SCULPT包含两个关键组件：(1)拓扑感知的激活正则化器，在训练中抑制量化不利的偏斜度和峰度；(2)稳定的基于百分位的裁剪机制，学习部署就绪的激活边界。该方法在普通FP32微调阶段生效，不模拟量化过程，学到的裁剪边界可直接导出到标准PTQ工作流中。

Result: SCULPT能够直接导出学习到的裁剪边界到标准PTQ流程，支持INT8及更低比特设置（如W4A8）的低比特部署。相比QAT无需在优化中模拟量化，相比后处理后修复型PTQ无需运行时激活变换。

Conclusion: SCULPT提供了一种轻量的训练时途径，在不增加QAT复杂度的前提下改善激活分布形态，使模型天然具备PTQ友好性，显著降低低比特部署难度。

Abstract: Edge vision models are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware, making low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) attractive. In practice, standard FP32 training often produces heavy-tailed activation distributions whose outliers destabilize activation quantization: preserving the full range wastes quantization bins on rare extremes, while aggressive clipping causes information loss. Existing solutions typically rely on quantization-aware training (QAT), which adds training complexity and bit-width coupling, or advanced PTQ procedures that repair the model after training.
  We present SCULPT (Statistical Clipping and Uniform Loss for Post-Training), a training-time method that improves PTQ readiness during ordinary FP32 fine-tuning. SCULPT combines a topology-aware activation regularizer that suppresses quantization-hostile skewness and kurtosis with a stable percentile-based clipping mechanism that learns deployment-ready activation bounds. Unlike QAT, SCULPT does not simulate quantization during optimization; unlike post hoc outlier-repair PTQ methods, it does not require runtime activation transformations. The learned clipping bounds can be exported directly into a standard PTQ workflow for low-bit deployment, including INT8 and lower-bit settings such as W4A8.

</details>


### [10] [Swin Meets EfficientNet: Lightweight Architectures for GAN-Based Face Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01749)
*Sejuti Basu,Ashima Sood,Vijay Kumar,Sahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对GAN生成合成人脸的检测问题，提出了三种基于Swin-Transformer的检测架构，其中EfficientNetB0与Swin Transformer的混合模型在140K Real and Fake Faces数据集上达到99%的准确率和99.44%的召回率，优于纯Swin变体和CNN基线，证明层次化CNN特征与移位窗口自注意力结合是一种高效轻量的伪造人脸检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型（GAN、扩散模型、自回归系统）生成的伪造人脸图像与真实照片几乎无法区分，给身份盗用、欺诈和虚假信息传播带来严重威胁。现有检测系统要么依赖CNN（擅长局部纹理但缺乏全局上下文理解），要么依赖全局视觉Transformer（能捕捉长距离结构但计算开销大），亟需一种兼具效率与精度的检测方案。

Method: 探索了三种基于Swin-Transformer的检测实现：（1）从头训练的紧凑型Swin Transformer；（2）基于ImageNet-1K预训练的Swin-Tiny和Swin-Small模型进行二分类微调；（3）一种将EfficientNet-B0的卷积处理与Swin Transformer后端结合的混合架构。模型使用140K Real and Fake Faces数据集（含StyleGAN生成的伪造人脸，以及来自Flickr和DFDC的真实人脸）进行训练、验证和测试。

Result: 在5,000张测试图像上，EfficientNetB0+Swin混合模型取得了99%的准确率和99.44%的召回率，优于纯Swin变体和此前的纯CNN基线。

Conclusion: 将层次化CNN特征与移位窗口自注意力机制相结合，为检测GAN生成的合成人脸提供了一种高效且计算轻量的方法。

Abstract: Modern generative models, such as GANs, diffusion architectures, and autoregressive systems, now produce facial images that are nearly indistinguishable from authentic photographs. This capability makes detecting forged images increasingly difficult, raising serious concerns about identity theft, fraud, and misinformation campaigns. Our research focuses specifically on GAN-generated synthetic faces, which underpin many face-centric deepfakes, and investigates efficient detection approaches using image analysis alone. Existing detection systems rely heavily on either convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or global vision transformers. While CNNs excel at identifying texture-based local features, they struggle with broader contextual understanding. Traditional Vision Transformer (ViT) models can capture long-range structures effectively, but demand substantial computational resources. Our work explores Swin-Transformer-based architectures across three implementations: a compact Swin Transformer trained from the ground up, ImageNet-1K pre-trained Swin-Tiny and Swin-Small models adapted for binary classification, and a novel hybrid combining EfficientNet-B0's convolutional processing with a Swin Transformer backend. We evaluated all models using the 140K Real and Fake Faces dataset, which includes StyleGAN-generated fake faces alongside authentic images from Flickr and DFDC, with balanced splits for training, validation, and testing. The EfficientNetB0+Swin hybrid achieved 99% accuracy and a 99.44% recall on 5,000 test images, outperforming both pure Swin variants and a previous CNN-only baseline on this dataset. Our results suggest that combining hierarchical CNN features with shifted-window self-attention provides an efficient and computationally lightweight method for detecting GAN-generated synthetic faces.

</details>


### [11] [AlphaRAD: Grounded Zero-Shot Classification in Chest Radiology via $α$-Corrected Binary Cross Entropy and Factorized Latent Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01757)
*Jianzhong You,Yuan Gao,Chris McIntosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaRAD框架，包含两个核心贡献：一是利用大语言模型从医学报告中解析大规模结构化医学概念空间，通过α修正的二元交叉熵损失进行对比学习，减少批次内噪声；二是提出FLaS（因子化潜在监督）模块，将视觉-语言预训练模型的表示分解为独立子空间并施加对齐监督，在不增加参数的情况下增强空间定位能力。AlphaRAD在16个分类基准上达到平均最优性能，在7个定位/短语定位和3个分割数据集上分别取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练模型（VLPMs）为开放词汇胸部放射学理解提供了可扩展路径，但有两个方面尚未充分探索：一是医学报告中提取的结构化临床语义如何减少对比学习中的批次内噪声；二是如何设计跨模态融合以在无需增加复杂性的情况下实现更忠实于空间定位的能力。

Method: AlphaRAD通过两大贡献解决上述问题：(1) 利用大语言模型解析医学报告，构建大规模结构化医学概念空间用于训练，消除对比学习中的启发式配对匹配并减少批次内学习噪声，将模型定位为通过α修正的二元交叉熵训练的医学概念判别器；(2) 提出FLaS（因子化潜在监督）模块，将VLPM表示分解为独立子空间，通过专门的alignment监督增强空间定位的表达力，且不引入额外模型参数。

Result: 经过大量实证验证，AlphaRAD在多样胸部放射学任务中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。具体而言，在16个分类基准上达到平均性能最优，在7个定位/短语定位数据集和3个分割数据集上分别取得了显著的个体最优结果。

Conclusion: AlphaRAD通过结构化医学概念空间训练和轻量级因子化潜在监督融合机制，有效提升了视觉-语言预训练模型在胸部放射学领域的开放词汇理解与空间定位能力，并在多个任务类别上取得了最先进的性能表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language Pretrained Models (VLPMs) offer a scalable path to open-vocabulary chest radiology understanding, yet two aspects remain underexplored: how structured clinical semantics extracted from medical reports can reduce in-batch noise during contrastive learning, and how cross-modal fusion can be designed to produce more faithful spatial grounding without added complexity. We introduce AlphaRAD, addressing these opportunities through two contributions. First, we construct a large-scale structured medical concept space from medical reports parsed by a Large Language Model for training, thereby mitigating in-batch learning noise and removing heuristic pair matching in contrastive learning, and thus naturally positioning AlphaRAD as a medical concept discriminator trained via $α$-Corrected Binary Cross-Entropy. Second, we propose FLaS (Factorized Latent Supervision), an extremely simple yet effective cross-modal feature fusion module that factorizes VLPM representations into independent subspaces, using dedicated alignment supervision to enhance the expressiveness of spatial grounding without introducing additional model parameters. Through extensive empirical validation, AlphaRAD shows strong zero-shot generalization across diverse chest radiology tasks. Notably, it establishes state-of-the-art average performance across 16 classification benchmarks, while achieving individual state-of-the-art results via distinct gains on 7 grounding/phrase grounding and 3 segmentation datasets.

</details>


### [12] [Allocate Before You Embed: Adaptive Visual Input Allocation for Video Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01778)
*Song Jin,Zhongtao Jiang,Chenglei Shen,Huanxuan Liao,Haozhe Chi,Zhiwei Wang,Kun Xu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AllocEmbed的"先分配后嵌入"视频检索框架，通过在嵌入前将固定的视觉输入预算重新分配到更多帧上（而非固定采样少数高分辨率帧），在保持推理预算不变的情况下显著提升大规模视频检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视频检索方法通常以原始分辨率采样少量固定帧，这既限制了时间覆盖范围又忽略了帧的重要性。作者通过实证分析发现，在固定视觉输入预算下扩展时间覆盖范围能够提升检索性能，且保留原始每帧分辨率时提升更大，揭示了时间覆盖与空间保真度之间的互补关系。

Method: AllocEmbed采用两阶段框架：(1) 轻量级分配器利用低成本的预览帧，在嵌入骨干网络之前为每一帧分配不同的分辨率，从而在更多帧之间重新分配固定的视觉输入预算，在检索受益最大的帧上保留更多细节，在其他帧上降低视觉成本；(2) 提出检索驱动策略优化（RDPO），通过排名验证的相似度差距和置信度引导的效率激励，直接从检索反馈中学习分配器。整个方法完全在骨干网络之前运行，无需修改嵌入模型或下游流水线。

Result: 在MMEB-V2的V-QA和V-RET任务以及作者提出的LongRet基准上，AllocEmbed在预算匹配的比较方法中取得了最佳的整体检索性能，并且能够跨嵌入骨干网络进行迁移。

Conclusion: AllocEmbed证明了在固定预算下动态分配帧级分辨率而非固定均匀采样，可以有效扩展时间覆盖并提升视频检索性能，同时该方法是即插即用的，兼容现有检索系统无需修改嵌入模型。

Abstract: Large-scale video retrieval requires embedding models to encode long and diverse videos under tight visual-input and inference budgets. Existing methods typically sample a small, fixed set of frames at their original resolution, limiting temporal coverage and ignoring frame importance. Our empirical analysis shows that expanding temporal coverage improves retrieval even under a fixed visual-input budget. Gains are larger when the original per-frame resolution is preserved, highlighting the complementary roles of temporal coverage and spatial fidelity. Motivated by this finding, we propose AllocEmbed, an allocate-then-embed framework that reallocates a fixed visual-input budget across more frames. A lightweight allocator uses low-cost previews to assign frame-wise resolutions before the embedding backbone, preserving more detail where it most benefits retrieval while reducing visual cost elsewhere. We further introduce Retrieval-Driven Policy Optimization (RDPO), which learns the allocator directly from retrieval feedback using a rank-validated similarity gap and a confidence-guided efficiency incentive. Operating entirely before the backbone, AllocEmbed integrates with existing retrieval systems without modifying the embedding model or downstream pipeline. Experiments on the MMEB-V2 V-QA and V-RET tasks and our LongRet benchmark show that AllocEmbed achieves the best overall retrieval performance among the evaluated budget-matched methods and transfers across embedding backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jinsong8/AllocEmbed.

</details>


### [13] [Ten Architectures, One Error: Shared Failure Modes in Hyperspectral Classification under Spatially Disjoint Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01786)
*Ehsan Faghih,Fatemeh Ashrafi,Marguerite Moore,Zahra Saki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有高光谱图像分类中普遍使用的随机像素划分评估协议存在严重的数据泄漏问题（测试像素紧邻训练像素），并提出了一种将空间间隔与模型感受野关联的无泄漏评估协议。在十种不同架构上的实验表明，Macro-F1平均下降0.147，模型排名最多变动5位，且所有架构都倾向于在相同像素上犯错，表明数据本身存在光谱歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类领域广泛使用Salinas数据集随机划分方式比较不同架构，但随机划分导致大量测试像素紧邻训练像素，造成准确率虚高。现有基准评估存在泄漏问题，需要提出更严格的评估协议来公平比较各类模型。

Method: 提出一种将空间间隔与模型感受野关联的无泄漏评估协议。将协议应用于涵盖经典、光谱、光谱-空间、Transformer、视觉骨干和状态空间六大类共十种不同架构，在Salinas数据集上进行系统性评估与比较。同时分析各模型错误模式的空间分布与一致性。

Result: 无泄漏评估下Macro-F1平均下降0.147；模型排名最多变动5位；无泄漏评估限制了可测试的架构范围（需报告划分半径与感受野）；十种架构在相同像素上大规模犯错，指向数据中无法解决的频谱歧义问题。

Conclusion: 随机像素划分会显著高估高光谱图像分类性能，领域需要采用与感受野匹配的无泄漏评估协议，并在报告中明确划分半径，以实现公平的比较。此外，数据本身的光谱歧义是制约所有架构性能的共性瓶颈，需要从数据层面而非模型层面解决。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image classification still relies heavily on random pixel splits within a single scene. The Salinas dataset, randomly split, is among the most widely used datasets for comparing different architectures. However, under a random split method, a large fraction of test pixels fall immediately adjacent to a training pixel, which inflates reported accuracy. This work introduces a leakage-free evaluation protocol linking spatial separation to the model's receptive field. Applying this protocol across ten different architectures, including classical, spectral, spectral-spatial, transformer, vision-backbone, and state-space families, shows that Macro-F1 drops by 0.147 on average and model rankings change by as many as five places. Furthermore, leakage-free evaluation limits which architectures can be tested on a given benchmark. Since each partition supports patches only within a finite radius, reporting this radius alongside the receptive field is essential for fair comparison. In addition, this study reveals that all ten architectures misclassify largely the same pixels, pointing to a spectral ambiguity in the data that none of them resolves.

</details>


### [14] [CoViT: Instance-Correspondence Contrastive Learning for Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01787)
*Yisen Wang,Zhirong Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoViT（对比视觉Transformer），一种自监督学习框架，通过几何引导的对比学习为ViT注入实例感知能力，使其在目标检测和实例分割等实例级任务中取得超过2个AP点的稳定性能提升，且无需额外解码器或标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer（ViT）在语义理解方面表现出色，但无法区分不同对象实例（例如两只狗会产生相同的嵌入表示），这严重限制了其在目标检测和实例分割等实例级任务中的应用。

Method: CoViT通过两个关键组件构建三元组来实现几何引导的对比学习：(1) 注意力引导掩码——利用自适应阈值和形态学操作对多头注意力进行细化以生成实例掩码，识别前景锚点；(2) 最难对比挖掘——为每个锚点在掩码内选择实例内最难正样本（相似度最低的patch），从其他实例选择实例间最难负样本（相似度最高的patch），并在负样本搜索期间屏蔽实例内区域。这些三元组驱动对比损失，同时压缩实例内方差并扩大实例间间隔。

Result: CoViT在多个实例级感知任务中，以ViT作为骨干网络时稳定实现了超过2个AP点的性能提升，且无需额外解码器或人工标签。

Conclusion: 纯ViT能够通过固有的注意力先验和有目标的对比约束学习到实例感知的表征，证明了ViT在实例级任务上的潜力，无需额外网络组件或监督信号。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViT) excel in semantic understanding but fail to discriminate between object instances (e.g., identical embeddings for two dogs), limiting their use in instance-level tasks such as object detection and instance segmentation. We propose Contrastive Vision Transformer (CoViT), a self-supervised learning framework that injects instance-awareness into ViT through geometry-guided contrastive learning. CoViT uniquely coordinates ViT's attention maps and embeddings by constructing triplets: (1) Attention-guided masking: Refine multi-head attention via adaptive thresholding and morphological operations to generate instance masks, identifying foreground anchors; (2) Hardest contrastive mining: For each anchor, computing pairwise embedding similarities to select the intra-instance hardest positive (least similar patch within its mask) and inter-instance hardest negative (most similar patch from other instances), with intra-instance regions masked during negative search. These triplets drive a contrastive loss that simultaneously compresses intra-instance variance and expands inter-instance margins, forcing ViT to discern subtle geometric and appearance differences between instances. CoViT consistently achieves stable performance gains of over 2 AP points across multiple instance-level perception tasks by using ViT as backbone architecture. Notably, CoViT requires no extra decoders or labels, demonstrating that a pure ViT can learn instance-aware representations via inherent attention priors and targeted contrastive constraints. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [15] [DESA-TTA: Dynamic EMA and Source Anchoring for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01795)
*Atif Belal,Lilian Hollard,Marco Pedersoli,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DESA-TTA方法，通过动态时间平均和源锚定来联合调控mean-teacher测试时自适应中的教师更新和学生漂移，在视觉语言目标检测器的分布偏移场景下显著提升鲁棒性并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言目标检测器在部署时容易受分布偏移影响，而mean-teacher测试时自适应方法虽能提升鲁棒性，但对固定EMA系数高度敏感，且噪声伪标签的重复优化会导致学生模型累积漂移。

Method: DESA-TTA包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态时间平均——利用伪标签置信度和框密度估计教师不确定性，据此在教师参数漂移确定的边界内选择逐样本EMA系数；(2) 源锚定——根据学生漂移程度动态调整强度，将更新后的学生参数部分恢复至预训练值。

Result: 在多种分布偏移场景和两种VLOD架构上的实验表明，DESA-TTA一致优于现有TTA方法。在VOC-C上相对零样本推理提升AP50达14.5个百分点，且在YOLO-World上推理吞吐量比此前最优TTA方法高55%。

Conclusion: DESA-TTA通过动态EMA系数选择和源锚定机制，在低额外开销下有效解决了mean-teacher TTA中的教师更新敏感性和学生累积漂移问题，为VLOD的部署鲁棒性提供了实用且高效的TTA方案。

Abstract: Vision-language object detectors (VLODs) achieve strong zero-shot performance but remain vulnerable to distribution shifts during deployment. Mean-teacher methods for test-time adaptation (TTA) can improve robustness by updating a student model using teacher-generated pseudo-labels. However, mean-teacher TTA is highly sensitive to the choice of a fixed exponential moving average (EMA) coefficient for teacher updates, and repeated optimization with noisy pseudo-labels can cause cumulative student drift. We propose Dynamic EMA and Source Anchoring for TTA (DESA-TTA), a low-overhead method that jointly regulates teacher updates and student drift through dynamic temporal averaging and source anchoring. Dynamic temporal averaging estimates teacher uncertainty from pseudo-label confidence and box density and uses it to select a sample-wise EMA coefficient within bounds determined by teacher parameter drift. Source anchoring partially restores the updated student parameters toward their pretrained values, with the anchoring strength increasing according to student drift. Experiments across diverse distribution shifts and two VLOD architectures show consistent improvements over existing TTA methods. On VOC-C, DESA-TTA improves AP$_{50}$ by 14.5 points over zero-shot inference while achieving 55\% higher inference throughput than the previous state-of-the-art TTA method for YOLO-World. Our code: https://github.com/imatif17/DESA-TTA

</details>


### [16] [Improved Automatic Target Recognition in Synthetic Aperture Sonar Imagery Using Large Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01800)
*C. J. Moore,Alex Hurt,Jordan Malof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了现代CNN与Transformer架构在合成孔径声纳自动目标识别（SAS-ATR）中的性能，探讨了网络规模、架构选择、预训练方法、数据增强及正则化策略对识别效果的影响，旨在为训练最先进的SAS-ATR模型提供指导路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAS-ATR领域目前以CNN架构为主导，而Transformer在通用计算机视觉中已占据领先地位，但在该领域的应用研究较少。同时，受标注训练数据匮乏的限制，如何有效利用数据增强和跨模态预训练等方法尚未得到充分验证，需要系统比较不同架构和训练配置以获得最优性能。

Method: 对CNN和Transformer两类现代深度神经网络进行系统对比实验，考察网络尺寸、架构类型、预训练方式、数据增强和正则化手段等多个维度对SAS-ATR性能的影响，通过控制变量方式找出最优的架构与训练配置组合。

Result: 研究找出了在SAS-ATR任务中表现最佳的模型架构和训练配置，阐明了不同因素（如网络规模、架构选择、预训练方法等）对识别性能的具体影响，为后续研究者提供了可参考的训练路线图。

Conclusion: 作者通过全面的对比实验为SAS-ATR模型的训练提供了实用指导路线，证明了合理选择架构与训练策略对于应对标注数据稀缺挑战、提升识别性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) in Synthetic Aperture Sonar (SAS) is a task largely dominated by deep neural networks (DNNs). Most SAS-ATR models use convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures whereas transformer-based architectures have had much less representation in the literature despite being state of the art in general computer vision (CV) research. Additionally, researchers have had mixed results in attempting to overcome challenges presented by a scarcity of labeled training data by using methods such as data augmentation and the use of pretrained weights from a variety of imaging modalities. In this work, we compare the performance of modern CNN and transformer-based DNNs to determine which architecture and training configurations elicit the highest performance in SAS-ATR. We investigate how network size, architecture, pretraining method, data augmentation and other forms of regularization affect SAS-ATR performance with a focus on producing the highest-performing model and providing a roadmap for training state-of-the-art SAS-ATR models.

</details>


### [17] [Consistency as Regularization for Unsupervised Shadow Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01806)
*Anh-Kiet Duong,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowCLR是一个无需阴影掩码或无阴影参考图像的完全无监督阴影去除框架，利用阴影观测间的一致性作为正则化，在多个基准上达到与最先进无监督方法竞争甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阴影去除方法中，监督方法需要成对的阴影和无阴影图像，而无监督方法仍依赖阴影掩码或阴影参考图像。作者希望提出一个真正无监督、仅从阴影图像本身学习阴影去除的框架。

Method: 核心观察是阴影在不同观测中变化而场景内容保持一致，因此利用阴影观测之间的一致性作为正则化，鼓励模型恢复场景一致的视觉外观并抑制阴影相关变化。通过全局和局部一致性，进一步探索视觉上相关的图像，学习不对齐的观测，并聚焦于共享场景信息的表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，ShadowCLR在性能上与最先进的无监督方法相当，且在许多情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 证实了一致性可以作为阴影去除的有效正则化手段，且无需阴影掩码或无阴影图像即可实现高质量阴影去除，为无监督阴影去除提供了新方向。

Abstract: Shadow removal is an important preprocessing step for many vision tasks, yet existing supervised methods require paired shadow and shadow-free images, while unsupervised approaches often still rely on shadow masks or shadow-free references. We propose ShadowCLR, an unsupervised framework that learns shadow removal directly from shadow images. Our key observation is that shadows vary across observations while the underlying scene content remains largely consistent. We therefore use consistency across shadow observations as regularization, encouraging the model to recover scene-consistent appearance while suppressing shadow-specific variations. Global and local consistency further enable us to explore visually related images, learn from imperfectly aligned observations, and focus the representation on shared scene information. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ShadowCLR achieves competitive and often superior performance over state-of-the-art unsupervised methods, demonstrating that consistency can provide regularization for shadow removal without shadow masks or shadow-free images.

</details>


### [18] [Integrated Laser Scanning and Image-Based Topology Optimization Techniques for Detection and Quantification of Visible and Subsurface Structural Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01808)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Devin Harris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的非接触视觉方法用于结构缺陷检测与定量表征：一种利用高分辨率激光扫描生成3D点云来定位表面损伤并量化几何损失；另一种结合3D数字图像相关法与有限元模型更新及拓扑优化，通过表面变形响应推断次表面异常。实验验证表明两种方法均能准确识别和量化缺陷几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构缺陷的可靠表征需要同时解决可直接观测的表面损伤和无法从被检测表面观测到的内部损伤问题，现有方法在这两方面常顾此失彼。

Method: 方法一：使用高分辨率激光扫描生成受损钢试件的3D点云，通过测量点云与参考点云的对比处理来定位损伤区域、量化几何损失，并将缺陷几何传递到有限元模型。方法二：采用3D-DIC获取全场表面变形测量数据，结合有限元模型更新和拓扑优化的逆分析框架，从测量表面响应中推断次表面异常。

Result: 使用含受控光滑缺陷和随机分布缺陷的实验钢梁试件进行评估，与基于铣削的真实值测量对比表明，两种方法均能识别和量化缺陷几何，并为可见和次表面损伤评估提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 该组合框架为具有复杂和不规则损伤的构件建立了一条高保真、非接触的结构状态评估与模型更新的可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable characterization of structural defects requires methods capable of resolving both directly observable surface damage and damage that is not visible from the inspected surface. This study presents two complementary non-contact, vision-based approaches for the detection and quantitative characterization of defects in structural components. The first approach employs high-resolution laser scanning to generate three-dimensional (3D) point clouds of damaged steel specimens. Comparative processing of measured and reference point clouds is used to localize damaged regions, quantify geometric loss, and transfer the measured defect geometry to a finite element representation. The second approach combines full-field surface deformation measurements obtained using three-dimensional digital image correlation (3D-DIC) with finite element model updating and topology optimization. In this inverse framework, measured surface response is used to infer subsurface abnormalities through their influence on the spatial distribution of structural response. Experimental steel-beam specimens containing controlled smooth defects and randomly distributed defects are used to evaluate the approaches. Comparisons with milling-based ground-truth measurements demonstrate that both methods can identify and quantify defect geometry, while providing complementary information for visible and subsurface damage assessment. The combined framework establishes a pathway toward high-fidelity, non-contact structural condition assessment and model updating for components with complex and irregular damage.

</details>


### [19] [Video2Reaction: Training Foundation Video Models to Predict Audience Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01816)
*Sidong Zhang,Trang Nguyen,Shiv Shankar,Gauri Jagatap,Deepak Chandran,Andrea Fanelli,Madalina Fiterau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Video2Reaction多模态数据集，将短视频片段与观众在社交媒体上表达的情绪反应进行映射，以标签分布形式建模情绪的多样性和主观性，并通过实验证明基于LoRA微调的视觉语言模型在该数据集上能有效学习并超越专用基线，且预微调后可迁移至其他诱发情绪数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频诱发情绪数据集往往基于单一标签或少数标注者，难以捕捉自然情境下观众情绪反应的主观性和多样性。作者希望利用社交媒体评论大规模聚合不同观众的情绪反应，构建能反映情绪感知固有歧义性的数据集，同时探索视觉语言模型在此类任务中的表现与迁移能力。

Method: 作者构建了Video2Reaction数据集，从在线评论大规模聚合观众反应，将标签建模为离散情绪类别上的分布。在此基础上，使用LoRA微调两种视觉语言模型（VLMs），并评估其在主导情绪反应预测任务上的表现；进一步设计预微调-微调迁移实验，将Video2Reaction上预微调的模型迁移到VCE数据集进行验证。

Result: LoRA微调的视觉语言模型在Video2Reaction上能够有效学习并超越专用基线方法。LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B在Video2Reaction上预微调后，仅用VCE训练数据的1%进行适配便取得了0.682的top-3准确率，与在完整VCE数据集上训练的最佳报告结果相当。

Conclusion: Video2Reaction作为以分布标签建模情绪多样性的数据集能够有效支持视觉语言模型学习诱发情绪预测任务，且该数据集具有良好的跨域迁移能力，少量数据适配即可达到与全量训练相当的性能，验证了其作为预训练资源的价值。

Abstract: We introduce Video2Reaction, a multimodal dataset that maps short movie segments to the induced emotional reactions of viewers in the wild, as expressed through social media comments. Video2Reaction captures the natural diversity of emotional responses by aggregating reactions from online comments at scale, modeling labels as distributions over categorical emotions to better reflect the subjective and ambiguous nature of emotional perception. We benchmark two vision-language models (VLMs) finetuned with LoRA, showing that VLMs learn effectively from Video2Reaction and outperform specialized baselines on dominant reaction prediction. We further demonstrate that VLMs pre-finetuned on Video2Reaction transfer effectively to VCE, another induced emotion dataset with a different taxonomy and video domain. Notably, LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B pre-finetuned on Video2Reaction and adapted on only 1% of VCE training data achieves a top-3 accuracy of 0.682, on par with the best reported VCE performance trained on the full dataset. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/infofusionlab/Video2Reaction

</details>


### [20] [Kirin: Animal Motion Generation from In-the-Wild Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01823)
*Brian Nlong Zhao,Zhuoyang Pan,James M. Rehg,Jiajun Wu,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Kirin框架，从野外动物视频中重建3D运动序列并配以文本描述，构建了首个大规模四足动物的视频-文本-运动对齐数据集AiM3D，并在此基础上开发了文本和图像双条件引导的动物运动生成模型，最终通过图像转3D模型自动绑定和驱动3D网格，实现可渲染的动物动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物运动研究远落后于人体运动研究，主要原因在于缺乏高质量的动物运动数据。人体运动可在受控环境中捕捉，但对大多数动物物种不切实际，现存数据集规模小且领域受限，严重制约了动画等下游应用。

Method: 首先利用大规模的野外动物视频重建3D运动序列并配以文字描述，构建AiM3D数据集；在此基础上开发视觉引导的运动生成模型，同时以文本和图像为条件生成跨物种的逼真运动；最后利用现成的图像转3D模型对3D网格进行自动绑定和动画驱动。

Result: 构建了首个大规模、支持视频-文本-运动三元组对齐的四足动物数据集AiM3D，并实现了一套从视频重建到运动生成再到3D动画渲染的完整框架。

Conclusion: 该数据集与框架为大规模、文本与图像联合条件的动物运动生成与动画领域建立了新的基础。

Abstract: Understanding animal motion is fundamental to modeling animal behavior and biomechanics, yet progress in this area lags far behind human motion research due to the scarcity of high-quality motion data. While human motion can be captured in controlled environments, it is impractical for most animal species, resulting in small, domain-limited datasets that restrict downstream applications such as animation. To address this challenge, we introduce Kirin, a framework that reconstructs motion from video, learns motion priors at scale, and generates realistic motion that can be directly applied to animated assets. Using large collections of in-the-wild animal videos, we reconstruct 3D motion sequences and pair them with captions to create AiM3D, the first large-scale dataset offering aligned video-text-motion tuples for quadruped animals. Building on this dataset, we develop a visual-guided motion generation model that conditions on both text and image to guide the generation of realistic motion across diverse animal species. Finally, by leveraging an off-the-shelf image-to-3D model, we automatically rig and animate 3D meshes using generated motion, producing ready-to-render animated animals. Together, our dataset and framework establish a new foundation for large-scale, text and image conditioned animal motion generation and animation. Project page: https://kirin-ani.github.io/.

</details>


### [21] [SliceBridge: context-consistent repair of corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01827)
*Jiheng Li,Michael E. Kim,Trent Schwartz,Gaurav Rudravaram,Derek B. Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,Lianrui Zuo,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SliceBridge框架，利用整流流匹配（rectified flow matching）方法修复T1加权MRI中被局部损坏的连续切片区间，通过条件化于周围完整切片及其位置信息进行重建，并采用区间相关初始噪声和同步采样增强切片间的连贯性。在9,877个训练体积和581个外部测试受试者上验证，该方法显著降低了修复区间内切片间变化误差（32.9%-41.3%）并提高了SSIM，同时将下游分割模型产生的大脑区域体积估计中位误差从1.95%降至1.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构MRI图像常因采集、运动、硬件或重建效应导致连续切片区间被损坏，此类局部损坏会偏差下游形态学分析结果，但丢弃或重新采集可用图像成本高昂，因此需要一种能够修复这些损坏切片的方法。

Method: 将问题建模为图像恢复任务，提出SliceBridge框架，使用条件于周围完整切片及其相对位置的整流流匹配模型来重建损坏切片；通过区间相关初始噪声、共享流时间和同步采样来鼓励切片间的贯通一致性，修复后将区间插回原图像，其余切片保持不变。

Result: 在4个数据集的9,877个T1加权脑MRI体积上训练，并在581个外部受试者上评估。与独立重建目标切片的匹配模型相比，SliceBridge在修复区间内切片间变化误差上降低了32.9%-41.3%，且在每种区间长度下均获得更高SSIM；在受控损坏场景中，下游分割模型得到的大脑区域体积估计中位误差从1.95%降至1.05%。

Conclusion: SliceBridge通过显式建模切片间连贯性，能够有效修复局部损坏的MRI切片区间，减少下游形态学分析中的偏差，为不可重新采集的医学图像提供了一种实用的修复方案。

Abstract: Structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) images are sometimes corrupted over a contiguous set of slices, where acquisition, motion, hardware, or reconstruction effects leave a single slice or short interval inconsistent with its neighbors while the rest of the image remains usable. Such localized corruption can bias downstream morphometric analysis, yet discarding or reacquiring an otherwise usable image is costly. We formulate this as an image restoration problem: given the location of the affected interval, reconstruct those slices from the surrounding anatomical and imaging context. We propose SliceBridge, a framework for restoring corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI using rectified flow matching conditioned on the surrounding intact slices and their relative slice positions. Through-plane consistency is encouraged by coupling the slices within the interval through interval-correlated initial noise, a shared flow time, and synchronized sampling. The restored interval is then inserted back, leaving all other slices unchanged. We trained and validated the model on 9,877 T1-weighted brain MRI volumes from four datasets and evaluated it on 581 external subjects using clean interval withholding and controlled corruptions. Compared with a matched model that reconstructed target slices independently, SliceBridge reduced error in slice-to-slice changes within repaired intervals by 32.9%-41.3% across interval lengths and achieved higher SSIM at every interval length. In controlled-corruption cases, SliceBridge reduced the median error in regional brain volume estimates produced by a downstream segmentation model from 1.95% in corrupted volumes to 1.05%.

Abstract: Spontaneous movement is one of the earliest windows onto an infant's neuromotor health, and structured clinical instruments that score it are validated early predictors of cerebral-palsy risk. However, they require specially trained raters, are time-consuming, and carry inter-rater variability. This motivates automated, video-based markerless assessment, especially as marker-based motion capture is impractical in infants. Yet the foundation models that make markerless capture possible are trained almost entirely on adults: our recent multi-view infant study found that no single model is jointly best, with strong 2D keypoint accuracy and direct 3D body recovery split across different models. While that study identifies this trade-off, it does not resolve it. Here, we perform cross-model distillation from the Sapiens 2 pose model into the SAM 3D Body model, using unannotated infant video alone. A frozen teacher supplies dense pseudo-labels, and a differentiable renderer aligns the predicted mesh to them in the training loop. On eleven held-out infants (18 sessions, 173 recordings) under our prior study's multi-view protocol, fine-tuning improves same-view 2D keypoint agreement with the Sapiens reference (median body percentage of correct keypoints @ 10px 0.22 -> 0.42, face 0.22 -> 0.42) and Procrustes-aligned mean per joint 3D position error (25.5 -> 22.2 mm). This demonstrates how cross-model distillation improves SAM 3D Body model performance on infants.

### [23] [RAFT-DVC: Resolution-Aware Machine Learning-Based Digital Volume Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01876)
*Zixiang Tong,Lehu Bu,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RAFT-DVC，一种基于RAFT架构、具有分辨率感知能力的数字体相关（DVC）求解器家族（下采样因子s=2,4,8）。研究发现三个求解器在特征网格上定位精度约为0.017体素，原始体素误差约为0.017s体素。求解器在不同位移幅度和纹理条件下呈现互补的工作区间，在细纹理/小位移时与经典DVC相当，在粗纹理/大位移时更具优势。同时纠正了3D RAFT相关采样中的坐标顺序问题，并通过非立方脉冲测试验证了采样器几何正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习驱动的DVC模型中，内部分辨率如何影响精度和工作范围尚不清楚，需要系统研究不同编码器下采样对DVC求解器性能的影响。

Method: 提出RAFT-DVC家族，使用三种编码器下采样因子（s=2,4,8）构建匹配设计的DVC求解器；通过合成基准、频率扫描测试、拼接推理以及关键的非立方脉冲测试来验证采样器几何；在共聚焦压痕图像和微CT弹性泡沫图像上进行实验验证。

Result: 三个求解器均能达到约0.017特征网格体素的位移定位精度；细纹理小位移场景下与调优的经典DVC同量级误差，粗纹理大位移场景下具有竞争力或优势；采样器修正后提高了原生输入的准确性和对未见体素尺寸的泛化能力；在泡沫微CT上展示了跨纹理迁移能力。

Conclusion: RAFT-DVC是一个快速、分辨率感知的稠密DVC框架，其精度和工作范围已被系统表征；证明了求解器工作区间与变形幅度和图像纹理的匹配对于实际应用至关重要。

Abstract: Digital volume correlation (DVC) provides three-dimensional full-field displacement measurements from volumetric images, but how the internal resolution of a machine-learning-based DVC model affects accuracy and operating range remains poorly understood. Here, we present RAFT-DVC, a resolution-aware family of recurrent all-pairs field transforms (RAFT)-based DVC solvers with encoder downsampling factors s = 2, 4, and 8. Using a matched design, we find that the three solvers localize displacement to approximately 0.017 feature-grid voxel, giving an empirical raw-volume error scaling of approximately 0.017s voxel. The solvers exhibit complementary operating regimes governed jointly by displacement reach and volumetric-texture compatibility. Synthetic benchmarks show that RAFT-DVC achieves errors of the same order as tuned classical DVC under fine-texture, small-to-moderate-displacement conditions and becomes competitive or advantageous under coarse-texture, large-displacement conditions. Frequency-swept tests quantify deformation spatial resolution, while tiled inference enables dense estimation on large volumes. Evaluation on confocal volumetric images acquired during indentation illustrates the importance of matching solver operating regime to deformation magnitude and image texture. Tests on micro-CT images of elastomeric foam, despite training only on particle-labeled synthetic data, provide evidence of cross-texture transfer. We also identify coordinate-order inconsistencies in three-dimensional RAFT correlation sampling and introduce a non-cubic impulse test to verify sampler geometry independently of network training. Correcting the sampler improves native-input accuracy and generalization to unseen volume dimensions. Together, these results establish RAFT-DVC as a fast, resolution-aware framework for dense DVC with characterized accuracy and operating regimes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语识别与匹配任务中，词典中的孤立手语视频与连续手语视频中的手语实例之间缺乏有效的匹配机制，且跨语言/跨数据集的泛化能力不足。作者希望仅依据视觉相似性（手形与相对身体的运动）实现词典到连续视频的匹配，并支持未见手语的匹配。
