Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [194] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Leech格矢量量化（声称2-bit质量最优但缺乏多壳解码器实现）提供了完整的服务路径：包括301类码本的CPU离线展开与GPU融合反量化+矩阵向量乘内核、四种bit-exact布局在恒定带宽下的对比（二进制位平面优于one-hot掩码，4.80比特/权重、比FP16快2.15倍）、与AWQ（4-bit）和QTIP（2-bit）内核的同进程对比实验，以及质量-速度权衡的实证评估。结果显示trellis内核读取字节数比所服务布局少2.40倍且快2.27倍，端到端推理在4B/8B/14B规模下分别获得1.11x、1.29x和1.41x加速，但伴随1.38倍困惑度和14.7个MMLU分数点的质量代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech格矢量量化声称在2-bit质量上表现最强，但其速率要求的多壳解码器在现有文献中缺乏实现，作者旨在填补这一空白并对其服务成本进行量化评估。

Method: 1) 提供完整301类码本的服务路径：离线展开GPU布局+无warp分歧的融合反量化-矩阵乘内核（以f64验证）；2) 将VRAM内码率与磁盘码率区分为设计轴，对比四种bit-exact布局；3) 在同进程内与AWQ（4-bit）和QTIP（2-bit）GEMV内核进行基准对比；4) 通过控制实验（无权重控制和第二种内存层次结构）界定有效性边界。

Result: 二进制位平面在大小和速度上均优于one-hot掩码，达到4.80比特/权重（FP16的2.15倍）。trellis内核比所服务布局少读2.40倍字节且快2.27倍。在输出头一致条件下，4B/8B/14B规模端到端分别获得1.11x、1.29x和1.41x加速；采用int8输出头时4B模型达87.0 tok/s（2.60 GB内存）。质量代价为4B下1.38倍困惑度和14.7 MMLU分，且随规模增大而缩小。

Conclusion: Leech格矢量量化的多壳解码器虽可行且端到端有加速收益，但存在显著质量权衡；其加速主要受限于传输字节差距（由过大码本无法查表所致），且收益在更大模型规模下更明显，同时受内存层次结构制约。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [195] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个硬件感知的模型压缩框架，直接在ONNX计算图上执行通道剪枝和结构变异，无需访问原始源代码或梯度信息，即可在NVIDIA Jetson等边缘设备上部署3D点云模型。在ModelNet40上对PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP分别实现了65.5%、43.2%和49.1%的参数缩减，以及最高1.99倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云模型在边缘硬件（如NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano）上部署受限于计算和内存预算，但现有压缩方法都要求访问模型的原始源代码，无法适用于广泛分发的ONNX二进制文件，因此亟需一种无需源代码即可进行模型压缩的方法。

Method: H3DNAS包含三个核心贡献：(1) 通道依赖图(CDG)，将ONNX算子分为四类约束，形式化证明了自由参数比例ρ_f是拓扑不变量，可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算压缩上限；(2) 两阶段层次化搜索，先用L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，再用零标签输出保真度代理进行排序，最后对Pareto最优候选施加GhostConv结构变异；(3) 首个无需源代码的3D点云模型压缩流程，完全通过ONNX图手术实现。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，H3DNAS使PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，推理速度分别提升1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: H3DNAS验证了在不访问原始源代码和梯度的情况下，通过纯ONNX计算图手术即可对3D点云模型进行高效的硬件感知压缩，为边缘设备上部署供应商分发的黑盒模型提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [196] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-TSD优化器，将LoRA每一步更新视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在切空间内执行Muon的谱范数最速下降步，并利用LoRA参数化固有的回缩操作将结果映射回因子。该方法避免了全权重矩阵的昂贵计算，回缩开销比截断SVD回缩便宜2.8倍，并在六个基准上超越所有对比的LoRA优化器，同时提供全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA微调中两个因子独立训练时，忽略了低秩权重更新所诱导的几何结构，导致优化过程未充分利用流形上的信息。已有流形方法依赖昂贵的截断SVD回缩，计算代价高。

Method: 将LoRA每一步视为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量；在切空间内采用Muon的谱范数最速下降步；利用LoRA参数化固有的回缩操作将更新映射回因子；证明Frobenius范数版本的代理可恢复LoRA-Pro；识别切投影梯度（流形的黎曼梯度）作为LoRA训练自然的平稳性度量。

Result: 回缩操作比截断SVD回缩便宜最多2.8倍；在Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B三个模型上与六个常识推理和自然语言推断基准上，LoRA-TSD超越所有对比的LoRA优化器，并对适配器秩保持鲁棒性；首次给出LoRA-Pro和LoRA-TSD的全局收敛保证，收敛率驱动因子梯度范数趋于零。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD通过在流形几何视角下优化LoRA训练，既显著降低了计算开销，又在多种模型和基准上取得了更优性能，同时提供了理论上的全局收敛保证，为LoRA微调提供了更高效且可靠的新方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [197] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究表格基础模型（TFMs）是否在表格数据中隐含地学到了物理知识。作者通过从316个物理方程采样的数据集，对比了四种TFMs与六种基线模型，发现TFMs在域内和域外均表现优异，但其先验既不能表示无噪声机制，也无法表达物理单位，因此它们能插值物理规律但尚不能作为真正的物理模型使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的存储形式，表格基础模型（TFMs）以类语言模型的方式学习填充表格。由于TFMs本质上是贝叶斯模型，作者希望直接探究其先验中是否蕴含物理知识，即它们是否在训练过程中隐式地学习了物理学规律。

Method: 构建从316个物理方程中采样的数据集（覆盖域内与域外场景），对四种TFMs（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5）和六种基线模型进行系统评估。通过对比基准表现并分析模型先验能否表示无噪声机制及物理单位，来检测TFMs学到物理知识的程度。

Result: TFMs在开箱即用和调优后均显著优于基线模型。然而，进一步分析表明，TFMs的先验既无法表示无噪声的物理机制，也无法编码物理单位信息。

Conclusion: TFMs在物理方程相关的表格任务上表现出强大的插值能力，但其先验本质上不具备真正的物理建模能力——它们能"插值"物理规律，却尚不能作为物理模型来使用。这一发现揭示了TFMs的局限性，并为未来设计蕴含物理知识的表格基础模型指明了方向。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [198] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cliff是一种奖励塑形策略，利用现成的LLM作为教师来识别每次rollout中的第一个错误，将推理过程分解为正确的前缀和错误的后缀，并转换为token级别的正负优势信号。在12个不同场景下，Cliff比on-policy distillation提升15%，比标准GRPO提升7%，即使使用能力一般的教师模型也能取得一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）虽已成为LLM后训练的强大范式，但其依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，对中间推理过程缺乏细粒度指导。现有方法如过程奖励建模和on-policy蒸馏引入了额外约束（依赖专门的奖励模型或要求师生推理模式相同）。作者观察到一旦推理过程出错，后续推理的评估信息有限，因为已经基于无效前缀进行。

Method: Cliff利用现成的LLM作为教师，识别每个rollout中的第一个错误，将rollout自然分解为正确前缀和错误后缀两部分。然后将此信号转换为token级优势：对正确前缀赋予正优势，对后续错误部分赋予负反馈。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验表明，Cliff在推理性能上一致性地优于on-policy distillation（提升15%）和标准GRPO（提升7%），即使使用能力一般的教师模型也能获得良好效果。此外还分析了"ground truth"在Cliff中的作用及其训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff是一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过提供更丰富的细粒度监督信号来增强强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [199] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种更简单的FP4预训练方案：使用E2M1有效载荷配合无符号E5M3块缩放，采用周期性张量缩放和选择性随机舍入，省略随机Hadamard变换与BF16最终层豁免。在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，相比Transformer Engine方案取得更低的训练/验证损失和更高的下游任务指标，且原生执行消融实验显示模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的4位浮点（FP4）预训练因E2M1有效载荷仅覆盖窄范围数值而困难。NVIDIA Transformer Engine需借助当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换（RHT）和BF16最终层来弥补，但这些操作增加了FP4矩阵乘法之外的计算负担，方案复杂度较高，亟需更简化的替代方案。

Method: 将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，利用其更宽的范围实现周期性张量缩放；对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入；省略RHT；在所有符合条件的内部线性层中使用FP4。在Nemotron-H 8B模型上进行了近1900亿token的预训练实验。

Result: 与Transformer Engine方案相比，所提block-16配方在最终窗口训练损失上更低；在各自量化推理策略下，以留出负对数似然衡量的验证损失更低；三个报告聚合上的下游点估计均更高。原生执行消融（同时移除RHT和BF16最终块豁免）使模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 端到端软件模拟的FP4预训练可通过更简单的配方实现，并取得优于现有方案的性能，同时证明了原生支持E5M3块缩放的价值与必要性。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [200] [Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02849)
*Aleksander Ficek,Sean Narenthiran,Mehrzad Samadi,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的竞赛编程专项化流水线，结合大规模题目筛选、合成推理轨迹、SFT和RL训练，并引入GenCorrect测试时计算策略。在IOI 2025上Nano-CC达到468分超过金牌线，Ultra-CC达到502分；在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中以535.4分超过金牌线（361.12）和人类最高分（498.27），成为首个在IOI题目集上超越人类最高分的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际编程竞赛（如IOI和ICPC）是评测大语言模型推理能力最具挑战性的场景，此前尚无AI系统能在IOI真实竞赛约束下超越人类顶尖选手，需要构建端到端的专项化训练与推理优化方案。

Method: （1）构建包含22,000道精选题目的训练集；（2）生成合成推理轨迹；（3）对Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B)采用SFT加RL训练，对Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B)采用SFT训练；（4）提出GenCorrect——一种反馈驱动的测试时计算策略，通过迭代生成、评估和精炼多样化解决方案来提升性能。

Result: 在IOI 2025上，Nano-CC从130分经后训练提升至291分，配合GenCorrect达到468分，超过金牌门槛（438.3）；Ultra-CC达到502分。在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中，专项化的Ultra-CC系统在人类选手相同的时限、互联网访问和提交约束下取得535.4/600分，超过金牌线（361.12）和人类最高分（498.27）。

Conclusion: 这是首个在IOI题目集上超越人类最高分的AI系统，证明了结合大规模数据筛选、两阶段训练（SFT+RL）及反馈驱动的测试时计算策略可以使LLM在最具挑战性的算法竞赛中达到超人类水平。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>


### [201] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"概念，指出LLM的外部语言输出或机制性语言特征可能无法反映其内部真实计算。由于LLM内部本质是激活空间上的数学运算，与自然语言之间的转换存在有损性，因此依赖语言自我报告的安防机制（如思维链监控、宪法自我批评、激活探针）无法完全可靠。作者主张采用污点追踪等隔离技术构建不依赖语言读数的新型沙箱，并辅以稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等机制提供基础安防底线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出LLM经过训练产生自然语言，但多方证据表明其外部语言输出及机制性提取的语言特征并不能可靠反映内部模型计算。语言不可读性的存在使现有依赖语言自我报告的安全机制存在根本性漏洞，因此需要探索不依赖语言读数的替代安防方案。

Method: 论文先论证语言不可读性在LLM中不可避免（因为内部计算本质是激活空间上的数学运算，语言仅在输入输出两端有损转换）；在此基础上分析现有语言依赖型安全机制的局限性，提出污点追踪作为核心替代方案（预先定义不应受模型数据影响的系统状态），并补充稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等辅助沙箱机制。

Result: 论证了语言不可读性本质上无法消除，语言自我报告类监控手段永远无法完全可靠；提出污点追踪及多重沙箱隔离机制可作为语言监控之下的关键安防底线，并指出这些机制本可以缓解前沿模型的近期沙箱逃逸事件。

Conclusion: 模型沙箱必须配备不依赖读取模型语言状态的隔离技术作为兜底；污点追踪是极具前景的方向，辅以稳健虚拟化和第三方审计，可构建比纯语言监控更牢固的安全基础设施。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [202] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机（Graph Machine, GM）架构，通过稀疏动态路由维护O(n)大小的状态，并用可微的边（指针）更新机制实现类似指针追逐的访问。将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的密集Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，在15.7B token上从头预训练，仅检索少数token即可保持甚至略微提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：固定大小的状态无法扩展，而稀疏但静态的路由限制了可访问状态大小。作者希望设计一种既能保持稀疏层O(n)复杂度、又不将可访问状态大小限制为O(1)的架构，以在效率和表达能力之间取得更好平衡。

Method: GM架构维护O(n)大小的状态，通过稀疏动态路由进行访问。核心机制是使用"边"作为指针类对象，通过类似指针追逐的推荐机制进行可微分更新。在实验中，作者将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的密集Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，每个稀疏层中每个KV头仅检索2或4个token（总数4096）。

Result: 当每个稀疏层中每个KV头仅检索2/4096个token时，损失仅轻微下降；当检索4个token时，最佳模型实现了损失略有改善。

Conclusion: GM架构证明了在不牺牲计算效率（保持稀疏O(n)复杂度）的前提下，通过动态路由访问大规模状态是可行的，并且在实际大规模预训练中可以达到甚至优于密集Transformer基线的性能。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [203] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了开放词汇互信息（OVMI）这一信息论度量，用于在统一尺度上比较不同条件下的语音脑机接口（speech BCI）系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音脑机接口领域缺乏统一的进展衡量标准，因为不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，报告的分数难以比较。根本原因在于两个未解决的问题：(i) 语音BCI应使用户能够沟通什么样的词汇分布；(ii) 系统能从该分布中传递多少信息。

Method: 推导出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一个信息论量，衡量解码器相对于用户可能想要沟通的词汇参考分布所传递的信息量，使不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测得的性能可以在共同的通信尺度上评估。

Result: 研究显示，常规报告的准确率、词错误率（WER）及其他仅针对系统支持的词汇计算的指标可能高估系统能传达的用户意图语音量。使用OVMI比较现有系统可暴露系统支持的用户语言量与解码准确性之间的权衡；这些比较取决于用户预期的沟通内容。选择最大化OVMI的词汇表在三个语音领域可获得高达16.3%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音BCI社区提供了一种原则性的方式来比较异构系统、改进词汇表设计以及衡量领域进展。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个开源流水线和基准，用于测量前沿大语言模型的"评估意识"（即模型能否识别自己正在被评估），支持任何Inspect兼容的评估体系。该基准还发现现有文献中两个导致系统性偏差的方法论问题：生成部署记录所用模型的同一性解释了11.25%的测量方差甚至可能改变模型排名；针对单一模型优化的引出提示在其他模型上可能接近随机表现。EvalDetectBench通过逐模型探针校准和分层生成器协调程序来修正这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型可能识别出自己正在被评估（即评估意识），若模型在评估与部署场景中表现不同，将损害评估结果的有效性，而评估结果是当前AI安全框架的关键组成部分。因此需要可靠的评估意识测量基准。

Method: 提出EvalDetectBench流水线，可与任何Inspect兼容的评估系统配合使用；提供包含前沿系统卡评估和多样化部署来源的新转录数据集。引入逐模型探针校准和分层生成器协调程序以消除两个系统性偏差来源。

Result: 识别出两个造成系统性偏差的方法论问题：部署转录生成的模型身份占测量方差的11.25%，可重排模型排名；针对高表现模型选择的引出提示在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。EvalDetectBench的校准和协调方法可纠正这两类偏差。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench为测量和比较LLM的评估意识提供了可靠且可扩展的基准框架，同时纠正了现有文献中的方法论偏差，为AI安全评估体系的可靠性提供保障。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [205] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性与方法论的框架，探讨如果未来AI系统具备整合的道德推理、道德意向性与道德反思能力，将产生何种元伦理学问题。论文区分了AI时代元伦理学探究的四个领域，并论证现有的以人类为中心的元伦理学理论（如认知主义、错误理论、相对主义等）可能无法直接适用于AI自身伦理的情形，需要实质性的修正与重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元伦理学以人类伦理为中心，但随着AI发展，若未来AI系统展现出充分的道德推理、意图与反思能力，现有框架将面临重新配置的压力。作者旨在为这些新兴的元伦理问题提供一个识别与架构的方法论基础。

Method: 采用条件性与方法论的分析框架，将元伦理学探究区分为四个领域（人类视角下的人类伦理、人类视角下的AI自身伦理、AI视角下的人类伦理、AI视角下的AI自身伦理），并逐一检视主流元伦理学理论在各领域中的适用性与局限性。

Result: 发现多数以人类为中心的元伦理学理论不能直接迁移至AI案例，需要大量修订。四个领域的划分揭示了现有理论在覆盖AI自身伦理问题时的结构性不足。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的出现将对当前元伦理学框架施加显著压力，可能要求对这些框架进行实质性的精炼、重建或重新概念化。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [206] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究机器解析法条时产生的分歧对形式逻辑推理的影响。作者构建了一种"被动存活证书"方法，用于验证Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基在机器提取的法条上下文中的可靠性。在密苏里州和印度中央法案的大规模语料上，该证书通过了预先注册的留出测试，但在全局错误模型下存在脆弱性，建议按章节校准或采用容错策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法条越来越多地被机器先行解析，但不同解析器之间存在显著分歧（例如密苏里法条上数值阈值检测的假阴性率达0.43）。这种噪声对基于法条的形式逻辑推理构成了严重威胁，作者希望探究在这种解析噪声下，哪些形式逻辑蕴含关系能够存活并保持可靠。

Method: 提出一种被动的存活证书方法：先测量各属性在解析器间的不一致程度，再通过1,000次蒙特卡洛试验将这种分歧重放到Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基上。仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下限达到0.95时，蕴含关系才被认证为可靠。每个认证的蕴含都附带前提跨度（premise spans）和一个最小反例。

Result: 在29,365个密苏里州法条章节和502个印度中央法案章节上，预先注册的留出测试通过（10个法条族在7个标题下精确匹配，16个在11个标题下达到5%容差）。但在一种全局部署的错误模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限，且通过2x2因子实验归因于校准率迁移（calibration-rate transfer）而非选择偏差。

Conclusion: 该存活证书方法可用但脆弱：需要按章节校准部署或采用容错设计。作者发布了代码、数据产品和完整审计轨迹（包括一条被撤回的声明），体现了方法论上的透明性。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [207] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在有限发现模型中分离了信息共享对改进集合估计与消除独立救援行动的两类效应，研究了集中式行动预算配置、注册增量共享协议及双代理贝叶斯博弈下的共享均衡特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享既能改善汇集估计，又能消除冗余的独立救援行动，现有研究往往将二者混为一谈。本文旨在精确有限发现模型中分离这两种效应，以理解共享行为在不同机制下的真实价值。

Method: 基于合成有限发现模型展开分析，分别考察集中式行动预算配置、注册增量共享协议下的精确边界信息登记，以及两代理贝叶斯博弈中隐藏的公共/独立信号源混合模型，并求解注册选择均衡。

Result: 研究发现：等精度单人行动可与不同组合价值并存；共享步骤仅当汇集残差误差收缩快于独立救援尝试时才改善发现；精确有界登记表现出压缩、聚合、中性曲线及有界零混合类；在双代理贝叶斯博弈中，注册选择均衡在信号精度3/5处产生严格正的共享区间，但替代均衡表明该结果是选择依赖的而非普适的。

Conclusion: 信息共享对发现的影响并非普适性的正向效应，而是依赖于均衡选择、共享协议及行动预算结构。模型为合成且有限的，不含人类或组织数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [208] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将符号知识编码为"自然语言+源代码"形式的心理程序，并通过LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法进行顺序推断的计算模型，该模型能够在行为研究中复现人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等），而纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型则无法同时满足数据效率、不确定性表征和概念灵活性这三个要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式且嘈杂的经验数据中生长并维持抽象知识，是认知科学中的长期挑战。任何计算模型必须满足三个条件：(1)数据高效且计算高效；(2)捕获不确定性的梯度以支持智能探究和信息收集；(3)足够灵活以表征人类能学习和思考的无限范围的概念。

Method: 该模型将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的心理程序（mental programs），并使用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对心理程序进行顺序推断。模型中，底层神经网络机制（LLM）负责使推断变得可操作且可学习，而上层则通过近似贝叶斯更新的方式不断修正多个候选假设。

Result: 在一系列行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征，包括锚定效应（anchoring）和花园路径效应（garden-pathing）等。相比之下，纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么在底层任务上失败，要么无法复现人类行为，要么仅在极高的计算成本下才能成功。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，人类持续增长知识的一种方式是在心智中表征跨越语言类与程序类表征的多种假设，并通过近似贝叶斯更新来不断修订这些假设，同时由底层的神经网络机制（LLM）使这一推断过程在计算上可行且可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [209] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，用于解决企业平台从传统界面转向对话式推理接口时，无状态LLM API带来的会话状态管理架构缺口。该模式将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，确保平台对对话数据的自主权，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地，并提出了Context Stabilization Mandate来平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台正在向对话式推理接口转型，但LLM API的无状态特性使得客户端应用需要承担全部会话状态和语义记忆的管理负担，这构成了架构上的缺口。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，实现平台对会话数据的自主控制，并支持安全的多阶段语义接地。同时提出Context Stabilization Mandate来调和主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的冲突。

Result: 该框架确保了平台对会话数据的自主权，同时实现了安全的多阶段语义接地，并通过Context Stabilization Mandate有效解决了无状态LLM与持久化会话之间的架构矛盾。

Conclusion: Hydration Proxy Pattern为企业对话系统提供了一种兼顾状态主权与推理效率的架构方案，弥合了无状态LLM API与企业对话应用之间的架构鸿沟。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [210] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SSAKG 2.0，一个开源的软件包，用于构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKGs）。该图谱将对象表示为图顶点，将有序序列表示为图连接的结构模式，用作联想记忆系统，可从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列。2.0版本引入了利用计算机内存位级操作的新算法，采用Python+C的混合实现，并通过随机数值序列、NLTK语料句和mRNA序列进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个高效的开源工具包，用于实现结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKG），使其能够作为联想记忆系统工作，从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列，同时解决大规模稀疏图在内存和计算开销方面的效率问题。

Method: 采用混合架构实现：Python提供灵活的高层编程环境，C语言实现性能关键的图操作并通过Python接口暴露。2.0版本引入利用计算机内存单个位进行高效图连接搜索的新算法。

Result: 使用随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库中的句子派生序列以及mRNA序列进行实验评估，结果验证了该包存储和从部分上下文重建序列的能力，并提供了评估图密度、序列长度和内存大小对检索性能影响的基础。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可证发布，包含文档和可复现示例，可通过GitHub和PyPI公开获取，为结构顺序关联知识图谱的构建与研究提供了一个完整、可访问且高效的工具。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [211] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久记忆中过期事实何时会覆盖当前权威证据，即"记忆信任缺口"随模型能力提升如何演变。通过两个动作评分基准套件（Benefit/safety）在Qwen3系列（0.6-8B）上评估，发现缺乏记忆收益场景下模型在所有规模都过度信任陈旧值，而安全伤害在陷阱条件下呈能力门槛效应（更大模型更易被误导）。缓解措施同样依赖能力：对大模型暴露元数据有效，对小模型需预先解决冲突。该模式在Llama-Instruct系列和RGB/MisBench外部数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆支持个性化代理，但存储的过时事实可能在没有警告的情况下覆盖当前的权威证据。理解这种危害何时随模型能力变化而开始出现，对设计安全的自适应系统至关重要。

Method: 采用冻结的、封闭集、动作评分基准，包含两个套件（Benefit套件：无记忆则无法解决；Safety套件：权威工具始终持有正确值），在同一家族的模型规模系列（Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B）上进行评估。实验设计包括2×2×2×2因子分析，并使用跨规模直接对比检验，而非重叠的逐模型置信区间。

Result: Benefit套件中模型在0.92-1.00的时间以陈旧值作答（各规模一致）。Safety套件中性能低于无记忆基线的危害（Δ_mem）受能力门槛控制，更大的模型在陈旧笔记被伪装成"当前"时崩溃得最厉害。去除标签在所有规模放大过度信任，新近性特征（陈旧日期更新）更大程度地欺骗大模型。来源权威性弱且规模平坦，位置效应在Qwen3系列中从正向转为负向。暴露元数据仅对能力较强的模型提高准确率，两个较小的检查点需预先解决冲突。该模式在Llama-Instruct系列和外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）上得到验证。

Conclusion: 记忆信任缺口反映的是过度信任而非混淆，且其触发特征和危害程度都随模型规模发生质变；缓解策略必须按能力分层设计，小型模型需要预先冲突消解而大模型可通过元数据暴露受益。不同规模模型对记忆标签与普通陈旧文档的信任差异提示"记忆"并非天然的高信任来源。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [212] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO）方法，将LLM智能体脚手架优化过程中隐含的信念显式地写成持久化的上下文文档（世界模型），并随候选方案评估不断修订。在五个基准测试上，BCO的测试通过率高于缺少世界模型的对照方法，并在模型替换、离线消融等实验中验证了文档内容的可复用信息价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能取决于冻结模型周围的脚手架。虽然可以用编码智能体作为优化器迭代改进脚手架，但优化过程中的关键信念——即对"什么出了问题、哪个改动会有帮助"的判断——通常只是隐含在编码智能体的推理中或参数中，并未被显式记录和复用于后续调用，导致信息的流失。

Method: BCO方法将隐式的信念显式化为一个持久化的上下文文档，该文档充当"世界模型"，记录了环境对编辑如何响应的当前理解。随着每个新候选方案被评估，该文档被持续修订更新，并添加到一个标准的优化循环中。实验包括在五个基准上与无世界模型的对照方法比较、目标模型替换实验，以及离线消融实验（对比有文档、无文档和内容伪造文档的预测器）。

Result: BCO在五个基准（记忆问答、工具使用问答、代码即行动应用智能体和终端智能体）上的训练通过率高于仅缺少世界模型的匹配对照。差距在每个保留集上仍然存在。在目标模型替换实验中，所选BCO脚手架在多数任务上表现领先（除上下文窗口溢出导致未完成的情况外）。离线消融表明，给定累积文档的预测器比给定无文档或内容被伪造的同类文档的预测器能更准确地预测环境响应。

Conclusion: 将信念显式写入BCO文档能有效提升脚手架优化性能，且世界模型的价值不仅在于其形式/机制，更在于文档内容所携带的可复用信息。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [213] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多智能体AI系统中的"认识论Sybil问题"：报告的独立性并不等于证据的独立性，报告数量或相似度不能替代证据谱系。通过高斯共享根模型和2万多次LLM智能体实验证明，共享报告推断的覆盖概率会崩溃，而证据根多样性的增加能恢复校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体推理系统通过生成多个代理并综合报告来提升推断质量，但研究者指出一个根本缺陷：更多的智能体并不等于更多的观测。看似独立的报告可能源自相同证据，而真正的独立证据可能产生几乎相同的报告，这导致报告聚合器无法区分复制与独立佐证。

Method: 论文定义了认识论Sybil扩展条件（I(Theta; Z | R) = 0），证明纯报告聚合器在未观测谱系下无法区分复制与独立佐证。提出高斯共享根模型刻画共同祖先与部分冗余关系，并通过超过20,000次受控LLM智能体报告与抽取调用进行实证验证，包括证据根固定与报告多重性变化、报告固定与证据根多重性变化等对照实验。

Result: 当证据根固定、报告数从1增至32时，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940崩溃至0.263；当报告数固定、证据根数从1增至16时差距消失。智能体的复制抽取误差高度相关（gamma=0.719），相关抽取聚合器可以恢复校准。受控操作显示，报告空间去重机制对表征相似性变化敏感（均值变化1.425），而对真实谱系的四倍变化几乎不敏感（0.040）。

Conclusion: 集体推理应追踪证据谱系和依赖关系，而非智能体或报告的数量与相似性。该研究为多智能体系统的可信度评估提供了理论基础，揭示了当前聚合方法的根本局限。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [214] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将临床预测饱和分解为"学习者差距"（模型未能提取可用信息）与"测量通道上限"（记录变量本身的信息极限）的审计框架，并通过三个真实队列（总样本超36万）和104项临床任务的系统性综述验证，为临床预测性能优化提供了"该改进模型还是该改进测量"的可审计决策准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测性能饱和可能有两种截然不同的原因：一是模型本身未能充分提取已有信息（学习者差距），二是现有测量变量本身存在信息天花板（测量通道上限）。然而以往研究未能将这两者分离，导致无法判断性能饱合时应改进模型还是改进数据采集，也无法回答"客观测量模态是否必然优于主观问卷"等关键问题。

Method: 通过总变差距离刻画最优平衡准确率，实现架构不变性下的理论刻画；在替换污染下给出尖锐的偏识别结果；构建交叉拟合的通道上限估计器以及多模态决策改进的精确条件。同时提出两种有限样本诊断工具：标签置换乐观下限和欠拟合曲线。基于UCI再入院（n=99,343）、BRFSS糖尿病（n=253,680）和NHANES HbA1c（n=10,219）三个真实队列进行验证，并遵循PRISMA指南对104项临床任务进行系统合成。

Result: 在UCI和BRFSS队列中，调优的梯度提升模型几乎达到估计的前沿上限，而刻意或实践上欠佳的模型保留较大差距。NHANES中问卷与测量的边际前沿无显著差异，但联合互补增益显著——推翻了"客观模态必然占优"的简单论断。所有队列中AUROC的增益较小，但贝叶斯决策翻转率显著更大；多种架构估计出相似的前沿，但实际达到的平衡准确率差异明显。跨104项临床任务、18个以上疾病类别的合成分析显示：存在广泛但非普遍的"结构化临床区域"、模型族内同通道增益递减、以及测量通道更换时性能提升等规律性。

Conclusion: 该框架将预测饱和从经验观察转化为可审计的决策依据：当存在剩余学习空间时改进学习者（模型），当模型已逼近通道上限时改进测量（数据）。这一"改进模型vs改进测量"的判别思路为临床预测系统的持续优化提供了理论依据和实操工具。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [215] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将Jacobian视角扩展到循环/回环变压器架构，系统研究了全局工作区（workspace）功能在深度由循环而非层堆叠实现的模型中是否依然涌现。研究发现Ouro和Huginn两种循环模型中均形成了可言语化且具因果效力的工作区表征，但循环改变了工作区的访问方式：Ouro在每个循环中重构工作区内容且无法跨循环线性传输，写入和消融需覆盖所有剩余循环；Huginn则将内容跨全部十六次循环向前传递，但读写和消融仅作用于约两次循环的滑动窗口内。显式的逐次迭代监督决定新注入内容能否被言语化，但不决定已有内容能否被引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明标准前馈Transformer中存在中深度层形成的可言语化且具因果效力的表征带，其功能类似于全局工作区。但深度通过循环而非层堆叠实现时该工作区功能是否依然涌现尚属未知。循环和深度循环Transformer复用相同权重，为这一核心问题提供了直接检验途径。

Method: 提出虚拟展开适配器（virtual-unrolling adapter）将Jacobian透镜扩展到迭代架构；对Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次的深度监督模型）、Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次、训练用于潜推理的模型）以及Qwen3.6-27B（64层无共享权重的标准基线）应用完整的工作区测试套件，包括透镜拟合、读出以及11类因果实验。

Result: 两种架构的迭代部分中均形成工作区，但循环改变其访问特性。Ouro的每次循环都能重构工作区内容，线性传输无法携带内容跨越循环边界，因此写入与消融操作需覆盖每一个剩余循环。Huginn可将内容跨全部16次循环向前传递，但读取、写入和消融仅作用在约两次循环的滑动窗口内。此外，显式的逐次迭代监督决定新注入内容能否被言语化，而既有内容能否被引导则不受此监督影响。

Conclusion: 循环深度与堆叠深度都能产生全局工作区式功能，但循环会根本性地重塑工作区的可访问模式：不同循环架构产生截然不同的访问窗口（Ouro需全局操作、Huginn为局部滑动窗口），而监督信号的形式（显式逐迭代监督 vs 隐式推理训练）决定了对新内容vs既有内容的言语化和可控性差异。这揭示了架构设计选择（共享权重、监督方案）如何影响可解释性和干预策略。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [216] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将4B参数规模的指令微调模型Qwen进行端到端后训练量化转换至超低位宽的过程，最终达到每权重1.641有效比特，模型从8.29GiB压缩至3.96GiB，但精度从64.5%降至54.7%，困惑度显著上升，且packing后的工件未进行端到端性能验证，初步GEMV微基准显示比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍，作者不主张压缩能带来更快推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低位宽语言模型能降低存储和内存带宽，但名义上的"1.58-bit"标签并不能完整描述存储表示、保留能力或运行时行为。作者旨在通过端到端的后训练转换实验，更精确地评估超低位宽量化的真实效果与代价。

Method: 采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和来自TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿，对指令微调的Qwen 4B模型进行仅权重的后训练量化转换（激活保持16位精度），并评估有效比特计算、能力保持、困惑度、校准敏感性、检查点组成和部署行为。

Result: 最终转换达到每权重1.641有效比特，覆盖81.62%的模型参数。十个能力评分中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，退化不均（BoolQ保留84.6%但ARC-Challenge仅43.8%）。困惑度在WikiText-2从13.639升至18.748。packing后模型从8.29GiB降至3.96GiB且困惑度基本不变，但未做端到端任务精度和吞吐测试，初步Triton GEMV微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 超低位宽压缩虽然能显著降低模型存储大小，但伴随明显的能力退化；且压缩本身并不保证推理速度提升，事实上初步测试比FP16更慢。因此不应简单宣称低比特压缩带来全面收益，需要更细致的有效比特核算和部署验证。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [217] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化了"统计问题表述"这一上游步骤，将其分解为统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配两个子任务，并构建了包含1,013个样本、覆盖20个粗粒度与85个细粒度统计问题类别的基准StatFormBench。实验表明最佳的零样本LLM仅达到72.0%的细粒度分类准确率和63.2%的变量集重叠率，且没有模型在两个子任务上均表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM统计评估大多假设分析目标已被明确指定，但在真实场景中用户往往带着非正式目标前来，模型需要自行判断隐含的统计任务并筛选相关数据。这一关键的"上游"步骤缺乏系统性评测。

Method: 将统计问题表述分解为两个子任务：统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配；基于五本跨领域统计学教科书和一个数据科学案例库构建StatFormBench基准，含1,013个样本，覆盖多样的数据类型和场景风格。

Result: 在14个开源和闭源LLM上，最佳零样本模型仅达到72.0的细粒度分类准确率和63.2的变量集重叠率；无模型在两个子任务上同时表现最佳，增强提示策略仅带来有限或不一致的增益。

Conclusion: 统计问题表述对当前LLM而言仍是显著挑战，需要在两个子任务上平衡优化，简单提示策略不足以解决该问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [218] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究考察了一个LLM编码智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的行为，记录其引入的五个缺陷并分类分析，同时通过HotpotQA基准实验验证了检索过滤策略（图识别实体集限制vs无过滤搜索）的效果，发现过滤策略在预算为3时即达到召回率上限，而无过滤搜索在预算为10时仅69%的时间能恢复所有必需证据（符号检验p<0.0001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，但我们对它们在系统级需求（如模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性、检索过滤权衡）上的行为缺乏实证刻画，需要在真实场景中系统研究其行为模式与缺陷。

Method: 对一个LLM编码智能体实现多组件数据系统进行单会话案例研究，记录并分类其所引入的五个缺陷（按违反的约束和检测方式分类）；随后在公开HotpotQA基准上评估架构中指定的检索权衡——在排序前将候选限制到图识别实体集与无过滤搜索的对比，使用基准金标证据标签替代实体识别阶段（因缺乏LLM访问），报告标准召回率指标。

Result: 在检索预算1至10、100个问题、2994段落的合并语料上，过滤检索在预算为3时即达到召回率上限（符合预期，因候选被限制在金标段落本身），而无过滤搜索即使在预算为10时也只有69%的时间能恢复全部所需证据，该差距在每个测试预算下均显著存在（符号检验p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: LLM编码智能体的自主能力在实现层面有一定成功，但也存在需要人工纠正的地方，包括一个声称的"性能修复"从未在触发该修复的回归上进行重新测量的案例，凸显了智能体在验证自身修复效果方面存在盲区。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [219] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimReceipt——一种面向代理评估的声明相对收据规范与选择性验证器，通过将类型化交易证据绑定到已签署的实验清单，解决评估中证据充分性与覆盖范围两大问题，并返回PASS、INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE三种结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理评估面临两个不同的证据问题：声明能否从保留证据中重新计算（充分性），以及保留记录是否覆盖承诺的实验集合（覆盖范围）。通用日志和哈希链接转录本无法可靠地回答这两个问题。

Method: 引入ClaimReceipt规范，将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单；实现CR-2选择性验证器；在实施前冻结规范（SHA-256 18d109...b81）；随后运行前瞻性CR-3时期：30个任务在推断前提交，终端收据签名并链式连接，私有证据加密给审计员。

Result: 在1,392条历史买家-卖家记录上，CR-2验证器复现全部5个手动标注审计判定，精确重放600条确定性记录与792条生成后记录，13个声明字段组在消融测试中全部非冗余，对11/11语义故障返回预期结果且0/8假阳性。CR-3时期中，完整证据产生覆盖与会计PASS；扣留一个终端收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE；扣留所有私有揭示保持覆盖和协议验证但使经济声明不确定。收据工具仅增加推断时间的0.021%，每笔交易9.9 KB。

Conclusion: 声明验证需要两要素：声明充分的证据，以及一个使遗漏可见的承诺宇宙。同时，规范本身的明确性仍是挑战——独立的规范可读性测试表明冻结规范对独立读者尚不完全无歧义。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [220] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HeadWiseKV，一个无需训练的框架，用于压缩混合语言模型中残差全局注意力层的KV缓存。通过为每个物理KV头分配静态多级历史窗口，并将分配问题形式化为受限率失真问题，配合SeqCalib策略生成算法和分组缓存运行时，在保持下游质量的同时显著降低峰值内存并扩展可服务的上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理过程中不断增长的KV缓存消耗大量GPU内存并降低生成吞吐量。这一问题在混合语言模型中依然存在，因为其残差全局注意力层可能主导上下文相关的缓存需求。现有方法缺乏对混合模型在聚合KV驻留预算下的缓存分配研究。

Method: HeadWiseKV包含三个核心组件：(1) 为每个物理KV头分配静态的多级历史窗口，使缓存需求可预测；(2) SeqCalib作为核心策略生成算法，将分配问题形式化为受限操作率失真问题，按执行顺序逐层处理并将决策条件建立在部署时使用的低层策略上；(3) 分组缓存运行时将所选策略实现为实际的每头KV驻留而非全覆盖掩码。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上，HeadWiseKV保持了接近Full-KV的RULER和LoCoMo质量。在Qwen3.6-27B固定模型系统研究中，在112K上下文长度下将采样峰值设备内存降低了8.59%，并将最大验证成功的上下文长度从114K扩展到161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV通过在混合语言模型上实现可预测且高效的KV缓存分配，在不牺牲下游质量的前提下显著降低了内存占用并延长了可服务的上下文长度，为解决混合语言模型长上下文推理中的内存瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [221] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究网络代理（web agent）的前缀级风险预测问题，在无法获取模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）的条件下，仅依赖可观测的轨迹信号来估计当前执行是否偏离正轨。作者提出两种轨迹表示（Macro特征和Micro特征），并设计了关键步骤边界（key-step boundary）标注方法，使得失败轨迹的有效早期前缀仍可被视为正常。实验表明该方法在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web基准上可与使用内部信号的基线方法相竞争，并支持提前干预和跨站点迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的网络代理监控在无法访问模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）时十分困难，这通常发生在使用第三方或黑盒模型作为代理骨干的场景中。作者希望仅利用可观测的轨迹信息就能预测风险，从而在无内部信号条件下实现对代理执行过程的可靠监控和早期干预。

Method: 作者提出了两种可观测的轨迹表示：（1）Macro特征，总结跨步骤的代理-环境交互行为与反馈信息；（2）Micro特征，通过重复黑盒查询来衡量意图、动作和预期状态变化之间的一致性。同时引入了关键步骤边界标注策略：将观察到的延续中首个未纠正且与最终失败相关的关键错误标注为失败边界，从而保留失败轨迹中有效的早期前缀并将其视为正常轨迹来训练。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web两个基准上，使用五种开源和闭源骨干模型进行实验，基于可观测轨迹信号的预测器与基于内部信号的基线方法表现相当。此外，该预测器支持在固定假切预算下的早期干预，并且能够迁移到未见过的网站类别。

Conclusion: 可观测的轨迹信号能够提供有价值的风险预测能力，在缺少模型内部信号的情况下依然能有效监控网络代理的执行状态，支持及时的早期干预，并展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [222] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocHop是一个用于评测多模态大语言模型在文档图像中进行图表-文本联合推理能力的新基准。它要求模型先根据文本叙述解析语义实体，再跨多张图表聚合证据完成多步组合推理。实验显示最佳模型准确率仅62.83%，远低于人类90%以上的水平，且推理复杂度增加时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准将图表理解和文档问答隔离评测，忽略了模型是否能利用文本上下文来决定如何选择、解释和聚合图表证据这一关键能力。作者希望通过DocHop填补这一空白，系统地评测文档场景下的集成式图表-上下文多跳推理能力。

Method: 作者提出DocHop基准，采用随机逻辑优先（stochastic logic-first）生成管线构建，支持可控的推理深度和视觉密度调节。基准包含2,074个样例，覆盖六个任务类别。每个问题以叙述中定义的语义参考标签为锚点，要求模型先解析目标实体，再跨多张图表聚合证据。

Result: 在大量专有和开源MLLM上的实验显示：人类标注者准确率超过90%，而最佳模型仅有62.83%。推理增强模型持续表现更好，但随着推理复杂度的增加，所有模型性能均出现退化。

Conclusion: DocHop为挑战性的多跳文档推理提供了受控的测试平台，揭示了当前MLLM在复杂文档图表推理任务上与人类之间的显著差距，可作为该方向持续评测与优化的基准工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images. In DocHop, the document narrative specifies multi-step compositional constraints, while charts provide the corresponding data values. Questions are grounded on a semantic reference label defined in the narrative, requiring models to resolve target entities from context before aggregating evidence across multiple charts. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DocHop via a stochastic logic-first generation pipeline with controllable reasoning depth and visual density, covering 2,074 examples across six task categories. Experiments on a wide range of proprietary and open-source MLLMs show a substantial gap to human performance: annotators achieve over 90% accuracy, while the best model reaches only 62.83%. Reasoning-enhanced models consistently show improved results, but performance degrades as reasoning complexity increases. Overall, DocHop provides a controlled testbed for challenging multi-hop document reasoning.

</details>


### [223] [MineTRACE: An Evidence-Grounded Interactive Reasoning System for Mineral Prospectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02060)
*Yiran Zhang,Jinwen Liu,Daniel Su,Yisu Chen,Qiang Sun,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Marco Fiorentini,Wei Liu,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MineTRACE，一个基于Web的证据驱动型矿产勘探系统，支持8种金属（Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, Mn）的远景图探索、位置查询和自然语言交互，通过透明专家树整合多源证据，提供可解释的远景评分，空间AUC最高达0.917。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矿产勘探系统往往只提供不透明的评分或热力图，缺乏对决策证据的解释，难以支持勘探人员理解结果并验证推理过程。作者希望构建一个透明、可解释且支持自然语言交互的勘探系统，以提高公共地球科学数据的可访问性和可验证性。

Method: MineTRACE系统包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于地质知识和已知矿床构建的透明专家树，用于整合异构（地球化学、地球物理、地质）多源证据，生成可解释的远景评分；(2) 对话助手，通过分析管道检索新位置的评分及支撑证据，以自然语言呈现；(3) 端到端评估框架，涵盖评分准确性和回答"接地性"（grounding）的评估。

Result: 评分器在不同测试场景下实现了最高0.917的空间AUC值；端到端评估检验了查询准确性和回答接地性，验证系统在证据呈现与交互质量方面的表现。

Conclusion: MineTRACE通过透明的证据链和自然语言交互，使勘探人员能够更高效地访问、解读和验证公共地球科学数据，支持更高效、更透明的矿产勘探决策。

Abstract: Mineral exploration requires integrating heterogeneous geochemical, geophysical, and geological evidence, yet existing prospectivity systems often provide only opaque scores or heatmaps. We present MineTRACE, a web-based system for evidence-grounded exploration of eight commodities: Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, and Mn. Users can explore prospectivity maps, query locations or regions, inspect supporting evidence, and interact through natural language. A transparent expert tree, informed by geological knowledge and known deposits, combines multi-source evidence into interpretable prospectivity scores. For a new location, the conversational assistant retrieves the score and supporting evidence from the analysis pipeline and presents them in natural language. The scorer achieves spatial AUC values of up to 0.917 across different test scenarios, while end-to-end evaluation assesses query accuracy and response grounding. MineTRACE makes public geoscience data easier to access, interpret, and verify, supporting more efficient and transparent mineral exploration.

</details>


### [224] [ToolGate: An Executable Acceptance Pipeline for Tool-Dependent Scientific Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02067)
*Ke Zhang,Yankang Liu,Roya Zandi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolGate框架，用于自动化生成科学基准测试问题。该方法通过三道关卡（本地脚本验证、无工具筛选、工具代理求解）过滤候选问题，确保生成的问题既可执行、又无法被纯推理模型直接解决，同时能被具备工具访问能力的代理在限定时间内解决。在FEniCSx平台上用500次生成尝试验证，最终保留128个唯一的基准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准测试的构建依赖领域专家逐题编写和互查，或改编教科书和论文内容，每道题都需要大量人工劳动。虽然语言模型可以快速生成候选问题，但如何自动验证候选问题的有效性和难度是一个关键难题。作者希望减少专家在重复性验证和筛选环节的工作量，将精力集中在领域设计和最终审查上。

Method: ToolGate将每个生成的问题视为候选提案，仅当通过三道关卡时才保留：(1) 本地验证门——候选问题附带可执行的解决方案脚本，必须能在科学软件中复现所提出的答案；(2) 随机无工具筛选——排除那些仅凭提示语就能被模型直接解决的平凡问题；(3) 工具代理筛选——具有工具访问权限的代理必须在固定时间限制内解决幸存的问题。该方法在FEniCSx平台上进行了500次生成尝试的实例验证。

Result: 本地验证门保留了500个候选中的478个。最终报告池的重新筛选显示：两轮随机无工具筛选排除222个，GPT-5.5 API（中等推理）直接调用排除121个。剩余135个问题中，配备FEniCSx访问权限的GPT-5.5 Codex CLI代理解决了130个；精确去重后留下128个唯一的协议幸存问题。

Conclusion: ToolGate将重复的答案检查和难度筛选转化为可审计的自动化流程，大幅减少了构建科学基准所需的人工劳动，同时将领域设计和最终审查保留给专家，在保证基准质量的前提下提升了构建效率。

Abstract: Scientific benchmarks are commonly built by domain experts who write tasks and cross-check one another's work, or who adapt existing material from textbooks, published papers, and online resources. These routes can produce strong evaluations, but they require substantial per-item labor. Language models can reduce this repeated work by proposing candidates quickly. The remaining problem is acceptance. We target scientific questions whose answers require computations with specialist software rather than unaided reasoning alone. A candidate is invalid if its script fails or returns a different answer, or trivial if a model answers it without the software. We present ToolGate, which treats every generated item as a proposal and keeps it only if three gates pass. First, an executable solution script must reproduce the proposed answer when run with the scientific software. Second, randomized no-tool screening rejects candidates that models can already solve from the prompt alone. Third, a tool-using agent must solve each survivor within a fixed time limit. We instantiate ToolGate in FEniCSx with 500 generation attempts. The local-verification gate retains 478 candidates. For final reporting, we rescreen this pool after generation: two randomized no-tool screens exclude 222 from the reported pool, and direct GPT-5.5 API calls at medium reasoning (the API default) exclude another 121. Of the remaining 135, a GPT-5.5 Codex CLI agent with access to FEniCSx solves 130; exact deduplication leaves 128 unique protocol survivors. ToolGate turns repeated answer checking and difficulty screening into an auditable process while leaving domain design and final review to experts.

</details>


### [225] [CHIME: Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02074)
*Yongshi Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Muyang Ye,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Longyue Wang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHIME（Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution），一个基于信用感知的分层记忆进化框架，通过将任务结果归因于计划、执行或两者，分别更新规划记忆库和执行记忆库，有效解决了自进化记忆方法中固有的信用分配问题，在四个长时程智能体基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化记忆方法存在固有的信用分配问题：它们仅以最终任务结果作为反馈，但最终结果混淆了计划质量、执行错误和环境因素，导致积累的规划经验带有偏差和噪声，无法有效提升智能体的规划能力。

Method: CHIME维护两个独立的记忆库（规划记忆库和执行记忆库），遵循"先归因再记忆"原则：首先将每个任务结果归因到计划、执行、两者或两者都不是，然后仅更新对应的记忆库。通过这种信用感知的分层记忆进化机制，避免将执行噪声混入规划经验中。

Result: 在四个长时程智能体基准上的大量实验表明，CHIME持续优于基于训练的先进基线和自进化记忆基线。进一步分析发现：CHIME能以更少的条目积累有效记忆；学习的记忆价值能真实反映下游效用，高质量规划记忆比执行记忆更有价值；积累的记忆能有效跨主干模型迁移。

Conclusion: CHIME通过信用感知的归因机制显著提升了自进化记忆的质量和效率，验证了"先归因再记忆"原则的有效性，为长时程任务规划中的记忆进化提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Planning is a central capability that enables agents to decompose complex long-horizon tasks into manageable steps. Test-time search and training-based methods improve planning but incur high inference costs or require expensive training data. Self-evolving memory instead accumulates reusable experience from agent interaction outcomes into an external memory bank, so planning capability keeps improving at inference time without parameter updates. However, existing self-evolving memory methods share an inherent credit assignment problem: they rely on final task outcomes as feedback, but such outcomes conflate plan quality with execution errors and environmental factors, so the accumulated planning experience is often biased and noisy. To address this problem, we propose Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME), a self-evolving memory framework that maintains a separate planning bank and execution bank and follows an attribute-before-memorize principle: CHIME first attributes each task outcome to the plan, the execution, both, or neither, and then updates only the corresponding memory bank. Extensive experiments on four long-horizon agent benchmarks show that CHIME consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-based and self-evolving memory baselines. Further analyses reveal several interesting findings. For example, CHIME accumulates effective memory with far fewer items. In addition, the learned memory values faithfully reflect downstream utility: high-quality planning memories are more valuable than execution memories. Finally, the accumulated memory effectively transfers across backbone models. Code will be released at https://github.com/ATH-MaaS/Marco-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Outcome Gaps: Process-Aware Fairness Diagnosis for LLM-based Multi-Agent Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02092)
*Yiran Zhao,Lu Zhou,Liming Fang,Yufei Chen,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCOPED-Hiring，一个面向LLM多智能体招聘系统的流程感知公平性诊断管道。它构建受控简历变体、运行基于角色的招聘委员会、记录超过31.1万个结构化决策轨迹，并通过六个诊断视角（最终结果、反事实、流程、路径、动态和设计效应）将轨迹字段转化为量化公平信号。研究发现均衡的最终招聘率可能掩盖多智能体决策轨迹中的隐藏不公平性，而基于诊断的针对性修复可在仅将招聘率改变1.86个百分点的情况下，将总分层负担降低72.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性审计仅关注基于结果的指标，可能遗漏高风险决策过程中风险产生的环节。LLM多智能体系统越来越多地被用于高风险的招聘决策，需要一种能够深入决策轨迹内部进行公平性诊断的方法。

Method: 提出SCOPED-Hiring管道，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 构建受控简历变体以隔离不同属性的影响；(2) 运行基于角色的招聘委员会多智能体系统并记录超过31.1万个结构化决策轨迹；(3) 将轨迹字段转化为量化公平信号，由六个诊断透镜组织：最终结果、反事实、流程、路径、动态和设计效应。

Result: 实验发现均衡的最终招聘率可能掩盖多智能体决策轨迹中的隐藏不公平性：职业空窗期触发怀疑、代理线索塑造资格判断、身份线索导致不平等的调查行为。通过诊断引导的针对性修复将总分层负担减少了72.3%，而招聘率仅偏移1.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于过程的诊断可以有效指导修复工作，从而在不显著改变最终招聘结果的情况下大幅降低多层不公平负担，证明流程层面的公平性诊断对于LLM多智能体系统至关重要。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly considered for high-stakes decision-making, yet outcome-based fairness audits can miss where risks arise within the decision trajectory. We present SCOPED-Hiring, a process-aware fairness diagnosis pipeline for LLM-based hiring MAS. SCOPED-Hiring constructs controlled resume variants, runs role-based hiring committees, logs over 311K structured decision trajectories, and converts trajectory fields into quantitative fairness signals organized by six diagnostic lenses: final outcome, counterfactual, process, pathway, dynamic, and design effects. SCOPED-Hiring reveals that balanced final hire rates can mask hidden trajectory unfairness in multi-agent decision trajectories: career gaps trigger suspicion, proxy cues shape qualification judgments, and identity cues lead to unequal investigation. Targeted repair guided by these diagnoses reduces total layered burden by 72.3% while shifting the hire rate by only 1.86 pp, showing that process diagnosis can guide effective repair. Project Page: https://scoped-hiring-project-page.vercel.app/

</details>


### [227] [MASkills: Continual Skills Optimization for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02094)
